# 4. VIEW MAP (TILEMAP)
# ==============================================================================
class MapViewerDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.setWindowTitle("Game State Views - Map (Tilemap)")
        self.resize(600, 640)

        self.current_map_img = None
        self._init_ui()
        self.render_map()

    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        top_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        top_bar.addWidget(QLabel("Screen Base Block (0-31):"))
        self.sbb_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.sbb_spin.setRange(0, 31)
        self.sbb_spin.setValue(0)
        self.sbb_spin.valueChanged.connect(self.render_map)
        top_bar.addWidget(self.sbb_spin)

        top_bar.addWidget(QLabel("Charblock (0-3):"))
        self.cbb_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.cbb_spin.setRange(0, 3)
        self.cbb_spin.setValue(0)
        self.cbb_spin.valueChanged.connect(self.render_map)
        top_bar.addWidget(self.cbb_spin)
        layout.addLayout(top_bar)

        btn_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        self.refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Map")
        self.refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.render_map)
        self.export_btn = QPushButton("Export Map to PNG")
        self.export_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4CAF50; color: white; font-weight: bold;")
        self.export_btn.clicked.connect(self.export_png)
        btn_bar.addWidget(self.refresh_btn)
        btn_bar.addWidget(self.export_btn)
        layout.addLayout(btn_bar)

        self.scroll = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.map_label = QLabel()
        self.map_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.scroll.setWidget(self.map_label)
        layout.addWidget(self.scroll)

    def render_map(self):
        vram = self.worker.get_memory_region("VRAM")
        if not vram:
            self.map_label.setText("VRAM not available. Ensure game is running.")
            return

        sbb = self.sbb_spin.value()
        cbb = self.cbb_spin.value()
        bg_colors = get_default_palette()

        map_offset = sbb * 0x800
        tile_offset_base = cbb * 0x4000

        map_entries = struct.unpack("<1024H", vram[map_offset:map_offset + 0x800])
        img = Image.new("RGB", (256, 256), (0, 0, 0))

        for ty in range(32):
            for tx in range(32):
                entry = map_entries[ty * 32 + tx]
                tile_idx = entry & 0x3FF
                h_flip = bool(entry & (1 << 10))
                v_flip = bool(entry & (1 << 11))
                pal_bank = (entry >> 12) & 0xF

                t_addr = tile_offset_base + (tile_idx * 32)
                if t_addr + 32 > len(vram): continue
                tile_bytes = vram[t_addr:t_addr + 32]

                for py in range(8):
                    for px in range(8):
                        actual_px = (7 - px) if h_flip else px
                        actual_py = (7 - py) if v_flip else py
                        
                        b = tile_bytes[actual_py * 4 + actual_px // 2]
                        c_idx = (b & 0xF) if (actual_px % 2 == 0) else ((b >> 4) & 0xF)
                        rgb = bg_colors[pal_bank * 16 + c_idx]
                        img.putpixel((tx * 8 + px, ty * 8 + py), rgb)

        self.current_map_img = img
        qimg = QImage(img.tobytes(), img.width, img.height, img.width * 3, QImage.Format.Format_RGB888)
        self.map_label.setPixmap(QPixmap.fromImage(qimg).scaled(512, 512, Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio))

    def export_png(self):
        if self.current_map_img:
            export_pil_to_png(self, self.current_map_img, f"map_sbb{self.sbb_spin.value()}.png", self.config)

# 5. FRAME INSPECTOR
# ==============================================================================
class FrameInspectorDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.setWindowTitle("Game State Views - Frame Inspector")
        self.resize(540, 440)

        self._init_ui()
        self.update_frame_display()

    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        top_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        self.step_btn = QPushButton("▶ Step 1 Frame")
        self.step_btn.clicked.connect(self.step_frame)
        self.resume_btn = QPushButton("Resume Game")
        self.resume_btn.clicked.connect(self.resume_game)
        self.export_btn = QPushButton("Export Current Frame to PNG")
        self.export_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4CAF50; color: white; font-weight: bold;")
        self.export_btn.clicked.connect(self.export_png)

        top_bar.addWidget(self.step_btn)
        top_bar.addWidget(self.resume_btn)
        top_bar.addWidget(self.export_btn)
        layout.addLayout(top_bar)

        self.frame_label = QLabel("No frame available yet.")
        self.frame_label.setStyleSheet("background: black; color: white;")
        self.frame_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.addWidget(self.frame_label)

    def step_frame(self):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.step_one_frame()
            self.update_frame_display()

    def resume_game(self):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.is_paused = False
            self.accept()

    def update_frame_display(self):
        frame = getattr(self.worker, 'latest_video_frame', None)
        if frame is not None:
            h, w, c = frame.shape
            qimg = QImage(frame.data, w, h, w * c, QImage.Format.Format_RGB888)
            self.frame_label.setPixmap(QPixmap.fromImage(qimg).scaled(480, 320, Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio))
        else:
            self.frame_label.setText("No frame available yet. Step 1 frame or unpause.")

    def export_png(self):
        frame = getattr(self.worker, 'latest_video_frame', None)
        if frame is not None:
            img = Image.fromarray(frame, 'RGB')
            export_pil_to_png(self, img, f"frame_inspect_{datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png", self.config)


# ==============================================================================
# 6. SEARCH MEMORY (CHEAT SEARCHER)
# ==============================================================================
class MemorySearchDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.setWindowTitle("Game State Views - Search Memory")
        self.resize(650, 480)

        self.candidates = {}
        self._init_ui()

    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        top = QHBoxLayout()
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Data Type:"))
        self.type_combo = QComboBox()
        self.type_combo.addItems(["1 Byte (uint8)", "2 Bytes (uint16)", "4 Bytes (uint32)"])
        top.addWidget(self.type_combo)

        top.addWidget(QLabel("Compare:"))
        self.cmp_combo = QComboBox()
        self.cmp_combo.addItems(["Exact Value", "Greater Than", "Less Than", "Changed", "Unchanged"])
        top.addWidget(self.cmp_combo)

        top.addWidget(QLabel("Value:"))
        self.val_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.val_edit.setPlaceholderText("Dec or 0xHex")
        top.addWidget(self.val_edit)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        self.search_btn = QPushButton("New Search")
        self.search_btn.clicked.connect(self.new_search)
        self.filter_btn = QPushButton("Filter Next")
        self.filter_btn.clicked.connect(self.filter_search)
        self.add_cheat_btn = QPushButton("Add to Cheat Engine")
        self.add_cheat_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4CAF50; color: white;")
        self.add_cheat_btn.clicked.connect(self.add_selected_as_cheat)

        btn_row.addWidget(self.search_btn)
        btn_row.addWidget(self.filter_btn)
        btn_row.addWidget(self.add_cheat_btn)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

        self.status_label = QLabel("Enter a value and click 'New Search'.")
        layout.addWidget(self.status_label)

        self.table = QTableWidget(0, 3)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Address (EWRAM)", "Old Value", "Current Value"])
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        layout.addWidget(self.table)

    def get_data_size(self):
        return [1, 2, 4][self.type_combo.currentIndex()]

    def parse_input_val(self):
        text = self.val_edit.text().strip()
        if not text: return 0
        return int(text, 16) if text.startswith("0x") else int(text)

    def new_search(self):
        raw = self.worker.get_memory_region("EWRAM")
        if not raw:
            self.status_label.setText("EWRAM memory not accessible.")
            return

        size = self.get_data_size()
        cmp_type = self.cmp_combo.currentText()
        target_val = self.parse_input_val()
        self.candidates.clear()

        limit = len(raw) - size
        for off in range(0, limit, size):
            val = int.from_bytes(raw[off:off+size], "little")
            matched = False
            if cmp_type == "Exact Value" and val == target_val: matched = True
            elif cmp_type == "Greater Than" and val > target_val: matched = True
            elif cmp_type == "Less Than" and val < target_val: matched = True
            
            if matched:
                self.candidates[off] = val
                if len(self.candidates) > 5000: break

        self.update_results_table(raw)

    def filter_search(self):
        raw = self.worker.get_memory_region("EWRAM")
        if not raw: return

        size = self.get_data_size()
        cmp_type = self.cmp_combo.currentText()
        target_val = self.parse_input_val()
        new_candidates = {}

        for off, old_val in self.candidates.items():
            val = int.from_bytes(raw[off:off+size], "little")
            matched = False
            if cmp_type == "Exact Value" and val == target_val: matched = True
            elif cmp_type == "Greater Than" and val > target_val: matched = True
            elif cmp_type == "Less Than" and val < target_val: matched = True
            elif cmp_type == "Changed" and val != old_val: matched = True
            elif cmp_type == "Unchanged" and val == old_val: matched = True

            if matched: new_candidates[off] = val

        self.candidates = new_candidates
        self.update_results_table(raw)

    def update_results_table(self, raw_mem):
        size = self.get_data_size()
        self.table.setRowCount(min(len(self.candidates), 200))
        self.status_label.setText(f"Found {len(self.candidates)} matching addresses.")

        for row, (off, old_val) in enumerate(list(self.candidates.items())[:200]):
            curr_val = int.from_bytes(raw_mem[off:off+size], "little")
            addr_str = f"0x{0x02000000 + off:08X}"
            self.table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(addr_str))
            self.table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(str(old_val)))
            self.table.setItem(row, 2, QTableWidgetItem(str(curr_val)))

    def add_selected_as_cheat(self):
        rows = self.table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if not rows: return
        addr_str = self.table.item(rows[0].row(), 0).text()
        curr_val = self.table.item(rows[0].row(), 2).text()
        code_str = f"{int(addr_str, 16):08X} {int(curr_val):08X}"

        self.worker.cheat_engine.cheats.append({
            "desc": f"Freeze {addr_str}",
            "code": code_str,
            "type": "Raw",
            "enabled": True
        })
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Cheat Created", f"Added cheat lock for:\n{code_str}")

# 7. VIEW I/O REGISTERS
# ==============================================================================
class IORegisterViewerDialog(QDialog):
    REGISTERS = [
        (0x000, "DISPCNT", "LCD Control & BG Modes 0-5"),
        (0x004, "DISPSTAT", "General LCD Status (V-Blank, H-Blank, V-Counter)"),
        (0x006, "VCOUNT", "Current Scanline Counter (0-227)"),
        (0x008, "BG0CNT", "BG0 Control (Priority, Charbase, Screenbase, Size)"),
        (0x00A, "BG1CNT", "BG1 Control"),
        (0x00C, "BG2CNT", "BG2 Control"),
        (0x00E, "BG3CNT", "BG3 Control"),
        (0x010, "BG0HOFS", "BG0 Horizontal Scroll Offset"),
        (0x012, "BG0VOFS", "BG0 Vertical Scroll Offset"),
        (0x040, "WIN0H", "Window 0 Horizontal Dimensions"),
        (0x044, "WIN0V", "Window 0 Vertical Dimensions"),
        (0x048, "WININ", "Inside Window 0/1 Control"),
        (0x04A, "WINOUT", "Outside Window Control"),
        (0x080, "SOUND1CNT_L", "Sound 1 Sweep Register"),
        (0x084, "SOUND1CNT_X", "Sound 1 Frequency & Control"),
        (0x0B0, "DMA0CNT_H", "DMA 0 Control"),
        (0x0BA, "DMA1CNT_H", "DMA 1 Control"),
        (0x0C6, "DMA2CNT_H", "DMA 2 Control"),
        (0x0D2, "DMA3CNT_H", "DMA 3 Control"),
        (0x100, "TM0CNT_L", "Timer 0 Counter Data"),
        (0x102, "TM0CNT_H", "Timer 0 Control"),
        (0x200, "IE", "Interrupt Enable Register"),
        (0x202, "IF", "Interrupt Request Flags"),
        (0x208, "IME", "Interrupt Master Enable")
    ]

    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.setWindowTitle("Game State Views - I/O Registers")
        self.resize(700, 480)

        self._init_ui()
        self.refresh_registers()

    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        top = QHBoxLayout()
        self.refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Registers")
        self.refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.refresh_registers)
        top.addWidget(self.refresh_btn)
        top.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(top)

        self.table = QTableWidget(len(self.REGISTERS), 4)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Address", "Name", "Value (Hex)", "Hardware Description"])
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        layout.addWidget(self.table)

    def refresh_registers(self):
        for row, (offset, name, desc) in enumerate(self.REGISTERS):
            addr_str = f"0x0400{offset:04X}"
            val_str = "0x0080" if name == "DISPCNT" else "0x0000"
            self.table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(addr_str))
            self.table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(name))
            self.table.setItem(row, 2, QTableWidgetItem(val_str))
            self.table.setItem(row, 3, QTableWidgetItem(desc))

# font_tools.py
import os
import json
import struct
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, 
                             QLabel, QPushButton, QComboBox, QLineEdit, 
                             QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QTabWidget, QSpinBox, 
                             QGroupBox, QTextEdit, QCheckBox, QScrollArea)
from PyQt6.QtGui import QImage, QPixmap, QPainter, QColor
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

class FontToolsDialog(QDialog):
    """GBA Font Ripper, Editor, and TTF Generator Suite."""
    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.setWindowTitle("Translation Tools - Font Studio & TTF Generator")
        self.resize(800, 650)
        
        self.current_font_img = None
        self._init_ui()

    def _init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        
        # Tab 1: 1bpp / 2bpp ROM Font Ripper
        self.tab_ripper = QWidget()
        self._init_ripper_tab(self.tab_ripper)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_ripper, "ROM Font Ripper (1bpp / 2bpp)")
        
        # Tab 2: TTF to GBA Font Generator
        self.tab_generator = QWidget()
        self._init_generator_tab(self.tab_generator)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_generator, "TTF to GBA Tile Generator")

        main_layout.addWidget(self.tabs)

    # =========================================================================
    # TAB 1: ROM FONT RIPPER (1bpp, 2bpp, 4bpp)
    # =========================================================================
    def _init_ripper_tab(self, parent_widget):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(parent_widget)
        
        nav_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        nav_bar.addWidget(QLabel("ROM Offset (Hex):"))
        self.offset_edit = QLineEdit("0x08000000")
        self.offset_edit.setFixedWidth(100)
        nav_bar.addWidget(self.offset_edit)
        
        nav_bar.addWidget(QLabel("Bit Depth:"))
        self.bpp_combo = QComboBox()
        self.bpp_combo.addItems(["1bpp (Monochrome)", "2bpp (4 Colors)", "4bpp (16 Colors)"])
        nav_bar.addWidget(self.bpp_combo)

        self.endian_check = QCheckBox("LSB First (Standard GBA)")
        self.endian_check.setChecked(True)
        nav_bar.addWidget(self.endian_check)

        jump_btn = QPushButton("Scan & Render")
        jump_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #2196F3; color: white; font-weight: bold;")
        jump_btn.clicked.connect(self.render_rom_font)
        nav_bar.addWidget(jump_btn)
        nav_bar.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(nav_bar)

        self.scroll = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.font_preview_label = QLabel("Enter ROM offset and click Scan.")
        self.font_preview_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.font_preview_label.setStyleSheet("background-color: #111; color: #00FF66;")
        self.scroll.setWidget(self.font_preview_label)
        layout.addWidget(self.scroll)

        btn_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        export_btn = QPushButton("Export Decoded Sheet to PNG...")
        export_btn.clicked.connect(self.export_ripped_png)
        btn_bar.addWidget(export_btn)
        layout.addLayout(btn_bar)

    def render_rom_font(self):
        if not self.worker.rom_path or not os.path.exists(self.worker.rom_path):
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "No ROM loaded.")
            return

        try:
            offset = int(self.offset_edit.text(), 16)
            if offset >= 0x08000000:
                offset -= 0x08000000
        except ValueError:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Input Error", "Invalid Hex Offset.")
            return

        with open(self.worker.rom_path, 'rb') as f:
            f.seek(offset)
            # Read enough data for 256 tiles
            raw_data = f.read(256 * 32) 

        bpp_mode = self.bpp_combo.currentIndex() # 0 = 1bpp, 1 = 2bpp, 2 = 4bpp
        lsb_first = self.endian_check.isChecked()
        
        tiles_per_row = 16
        rows = 16
        img = Image.new("RGBA", (tiles_per_row * 8, rows * 8), (0, 0, 0, 255))
        
        # Color mapping (Grayscale scaling)
        colors_1bpp = [(0,0,0,255), (255,255,255,255)]
        colors_2bpp = [(0,0,0,255), (85,85,85,255), (170,170,170,255), (255,255,255,255)]

        data_idx = 0
        for ty in range(rows):
            for tx in range(tiles_per_row):
                if data_idx >= len(raw_data): break

                if bpp_mode == 0: # 1bpp (8 bytes per tile)
                    if data_idx + 8 > len(raw_data): break
                    tile_bytes = raw_data[data_idx:data_idx+8]
                    for py in range(8):
                        b = tile_bytes[py]
                        for px in range(8):
                            shift = px if lsb_first else (7 - px)
                            c_idx = (b >> shift) & 1
                            img.putpixel((tx*8 + px, ty*8 + py), colors_1bpp[c_idx])
                    data_idx += 8

                elif bpp_mode == 1: # 2bpp (16 bytes per tile)
                    if data_idx + 16 > len(raw_data): break
                    tile_bytes = raw_data[data_idx:data_idx+16]
                    for py in range(8):
                        b0, b1 = tile_bytes[py*2], tile_bytes[py*2+1]
                        for px in range(8):
                            shift = px if lsb_first else (7 - px)
                            bit0 = (b0 >> shift) & 1
                            bit1 = (b1 >> shift) & 1
                            c_idx = bit0 | (bit1 << 1)
                            img.putpixel((tx*8 + px, ty*8 + py), colors_2bpp[c_idx])
                    data_idx += 16

                elif bpp_mode == 2: # 4bpp (32 bytes per tile)
                    if data_idx + 32 > len(raw_data): break
                    tile_bytes = raw_data[data_idx:data_idx+32]
                    for py in range(8):
                        for px in range(8):
                            b = tile_bytes[py * 4 + px // 2]
                            c_idx = (b & 0xF) if (px % 2 == 0) else ((b >> 4) & 0xF)
                            lum = c_idx * 17
                            img.putpixel((tx*8 + px, ty*8 + py), (lum, lum, lum, 255))
                    data_idx += 32

        self.current_font_img = img
        scaled = img.resize((img.width * 4, img.height * 4), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
        qimg = QImage(scaled.tobytes(), scaled.width, scaled.height, scaled.width * 4, QImage.Format.Format_RGBA8888)
        self.font_preview_label.setPixmap(QPixmap.fromImage(qimg))

    def export_ripped_png(self):
        if not self.current_font_img: return
        export_dir = self.config.get("paths", "exports") or "exports"
        os.makedirs(export_dir, exist_ok=True)
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export Font", os.path.join(export_dir, "ripped_font.png"), "PNG (*.png)")
        if filepath:
            self.current_font_img.save(filepath)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Exported", f"Saved to {filepath}")

    # =========================================================================
    # TAB 2: TTF TO GBA GENERATOR
    # =========================================================================
    def _init_generator_tab(self, parent_widget):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(parent_widget)
        
        # Setup
        setup_box = QGroupBox("1. Font Configuration")
        setup_layout = QVBoxLayout(setup_box)
        
        file_row = QHBoxLayout()
        self.ttf_path_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.ttf_path_edit.setPlaceholderText("Select a .ttf or .otf file...")
        btn_browse_ttf = QPushButton("Browse...")
        btn_browse_ttf.clicked.connect(self.browse_ttf)
        file_row.addWidget(self.ttf_path_edit)
        file_row.addWidget(btn_browse_ttf)
        setup_layout.addLayout(file_row)
        
        param_row = QHBoxLayout()
        param_row.addWidget(QLabel("Font Size (px):"))
        self.ttf_size = QSpinBox()
        self.ttf_size.setRange(4, 32)
        self.ttf_size.setValue(10)
        param_row.addWidget(self.ttf_size)

        param_row.addWidget(QLabel("Format:"))
        self.gen_bpp_combo = QComboBox()
        self.gen_bpp_combo.addItems(["1bpp (Monochrome)", "2bpp (4 Color)", "4bpp (16 Color)"])
        param_row.addWidget(self.gen_bpp_combo)
        setup_layout.addLayout(param_row)
        layout.addWidget(setup_box)

        # Characters
        char_box = QGroupBox("2. Character Set (VWF)")
        char_layout = QVBoxLayout(char_box)
        char_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Type the exact characters you want to generate in order:"))
        self.chars_edit = QTextEdit()
        self.chars_edit.setPlainText("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ\nabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz\n0123456789!?.,'- ")
        self.chars_edit.setFixedHeight(80)
        char_layout.addWidget(self.chars_edit)
        layout.addWidget(char_box)

        # Actions
        btn_gen = QPushButton("Generate GBA Binary & Width Table (VWF)...")
        btn_gen.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4CAF50; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 10px;")
        btn_gen.clicked.connect(self.generate_gba_font)
        layout.addWidget(btn_gen)

        self.gen_status = QLabel("Ready.")
        layout.addWidget(self.gen_status)
        layout.addStretch()

    def browse_ttf(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Select TrueType Font", "", "Fonts (*.ttf *.otf)")
        if path: self.ttf_path_edit.setText(path)

    def generate_gba_font(self):
        font_path = self.ttf_path_edit.text()
        if not os.path.exists(font_path):
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Invalid font file path.")
            return

        try:
            font = ImageFont.truetype(font_path, self.ttf_size.value())
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Font Error", f"Could not load TTF: {e}")
            return

        chars = self.chars_edit.toPlainText().replace('\n', '')
        if not chars: return

        bpp_mode = self.gen_bpp_combo.currentIndex() # 0=1bpp, 1=2bpp, 2=4bpp
        
        binary_payload = bytearray()
        width_table = []
        width_dict = {}

        # GBA Tiles are always 8x8. 
        for i, char in enumerate(chars):
            # Render character onto an 8x8 image
            img = Image.new("L", (8, 8), 0) # Black background
            draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
            draw.text((0, -2), char, fill=255, font=font) # -2 offsets typical TTF ascenders
            
            # Calculate variable width (VWF)
            bbox = font.getbbox(char)
            width = bbox[2] - bbox[0] if bbox else 4
            width_table.append(width)
            width_dict[char] = width

            # Quantize and pack into GBA binary formats
            pixels = img.load()
            
            if bpp_mode == 0: # 1bpp
                for y in range(8):
                    byte_val = 0
                    for x in range(8):
                        if pixels[x, y] > 127: # Threshold
                            byte_val |= (1 << x) # LSB first unpacking standard
                    binary_payload.append(byte_val)
                    
            elif bpp_mode == 2: # 4bpp standard GBA
                for y in range(8):
                    for x in range(0, 8, 2):
                        p1 = 15 if pixels[x, y] > 127 else 0
                        p2 = 15 if pixels[x+1, y] > 127 else 0
                        byte_val = (p1 & 0xF) | ((p2 & 0xF) << 4)
                        binary_payload.append(byte_val)

        # Export Files
        export_dir = self.config.get("paths", "exports") or "exports"
        os.makedirs(export_dir, exist_ok=True)
        base_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(font_path))[0]
        timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
        
        bin_path = os.path.join(export_dir, f"{base_name}_{timestamp}_{len(chars)}chars.bin")
        vwf_path = os.path.join(export_dir, f"{base_name}_{timestamp}_vwf.bin")
        json_path = os.path.join(export_dir, f"{base_name}_{timestamp}_vwf.json")

        with open(bin_path, 'wb') as f: f.write(binary_payload)
        with open(vwf_path, 'wb') as f: f.write(bytes(width_table))
        with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(width_dict, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)

        self.gen_status.setText(f"Success! Generated {len(chars)} tiles ({len(binary_payload)} bytes).")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Export Complete", 
                                f"Font packed successfully!\n\n"
                                f"Graphics: {os.path.basename(bin_path)}\n"
                                f"Width Table: {os.path.basename(vwf_path)}")

# graphic_editor.py
import os
import struct
import datetime
from collections import deque
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, 
                             QLabel, QPushButton, QComboBox, QCheckBox, 
                             QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView,
                             QScrollArea, QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QSpinBox, 
                             QGroupBox, QColorDialog, QTabWidget)
from PyQt6.QtGui import QImage, QPixmap, QPainter, QColor, QPen
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

def bgr555_to_rgb(val: int):
    val = int(val)
    r = (val & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
    g = ((val >> 5) & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
    b = ((val >> 10) & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
    return (int(r), int(g), int(b))

def rgb_to_bgr555(r: int, g: int, b: int) -> int:
    r, g, b = int(r), int(g), int(b)
    r5 = (r * 31 // 255) & 0x1F
    g5 = (g * 31 // 255) & 0x1F
    b5 = (b * 31 // 255) & 0x1F
    return int(r5 | (g5 << 5) | (b5 << 10))

def create_ips_patch(original: bytes, modified: bytes) -> bytes:
    patch = bytearray(b"PATCH")
    i = 0
    length = min(len(original), len(modified))

    while i < length:
        if original[i] != modified[i]:
            start = i
            while i < length and (original[i] != modified[i] or (i - start < 65535 and i + 1 < length and original[i+1] != modified[i+1])):
                i += 1
                if i - start >= 65535: break

            chunk_len = i - start
            offset = start
            if offset == 0x454F46: # Offset cannot be 'EOF' in ASCII
                offset -= 1
                chunk_len += 1
                start -= 1

            patch.extend(offset.to_bytes(3, byteorder='big'))
            patch.extend(chunk_len.to_bytes(2, byteorder='big'))
            patch.extend(modified[start:start + chunk_len])
        else:
            i += 1

    patch.extend(b"EOF")
    return bytes(patch)

# ==============================================================================
# GBA BIOS LZ77 (Type 0x10) Decompressor & Compressor
# ==============================================================================
def lz77_decompress(data: bytes, offset: int = 0) -> tuple:
    if offset + 4 > len(data): return None, 0
    if data[offset] != 0x10: return None, 0
    decomp_size = int(data[offset+1]) | (int(data[offset+2]) << 8) | (int(data[offset+3]) << 16)
    if decomp_size == 0 or decomp_size > 0x80000: return None, 0

    out = bytearray()
    pos = offset + 4

    while len(out) < decomp_size and pos < len(data):
        flags = data[pos]
        pos += 1
        for bit in range(7, -1, -1):
            if len(out) >= decomp_size: break
            if (flags >> bit) & 1:
                if pos + 2 > len(data): break
                b1, b2 = data[pos], data[pos+1]
                pos += 2
                length = (b1 >> 4) + 3
                disp = (((b1 & 0xF) << 8) | b2) + 1
                if disp > len(out): break
                for _ in range(length):
                    if len(out) >= decomp_size: break
                    out.append(out[-disp])
            else:
                if pos >= len(data): break
                out.append(data[pos])
                pos += 1

    if len(out) == decomp_size:
        return bytes(out), pos - offset
    return None, 0

def lz77_compress(data: bytes) -> bytes:
    decomp_size = len(data)
    out = bytearray([0x10, decomp_size & 0xFF, (decomp_size >> 8) & 0xFF, (decomp_size >> 16) & 0xFF])
    pos = 0

    while pos < decomp_size:
        flag_pos = len(out)
        out.append(0)
        flags = 0

        for bit in range(7, -1, -1):
            if pos >= decomp_size: break
            best_len, best_disp = 0, 0
            max_look = min(18, decomp_size - pos)
            window_start = max(0, pos - 4096)

            if max_look >= 3:
                sub = data[pos:pos+3]
                w_pos = data.find(sub, window_start, pos)
                while w_pos != -1:
                    match_len = 3
                    while match_len < max_look and data[w_pos + match_len] == data[pos + match_len]:
                        match_len += 1
                    if match_len > best_len:
                        best_len = match_len
                        best_disp = pos - w_pos
                        if best_len == 18: break
                    w_pos = data.find(sub, w_pos + 1, pos)

            if best_len >= 3:
                flags |= (1 << bit)
                b1 = ((best_len - 3) << 4) | (((best_disp - 1) >> 8) & 0xF)
                b2 = (best_disp - 1) & 0xFF
                out.extend([b1, b2])
                pos += best_len
            else:
                out.append(data[pos])
                pos += 1

        out[flag_pos] = flags

    return bytes(out)


class ZoomedCanvas(QWidget):
    """Enlarged pixel editor for the currently active tile or sprite."""
    def __init__(self, editor, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.editor = editor
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.drag_start_pos = None
        self.temp_draw_pos = None

    def get_zoom(self):
        w, _ = self.editor.get_active_dimensions()
        return max(4, 256 // max(1, w))

    def sizeHint(self):
        w, h = self.editor.get_active_dimensions()
        z = self.get_zoom()
        self.setFixedSize(w * z, h * z)
        return super().sizeHint()

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            z = self.get_zoom()
            x = int(event.position().x() // z)
            y = int(event.position().y() // z)
            w, h = self.editor.get_active_dimensions()

            if 0 <= x < w and 0 <= y < h:
                self.drag_start_pos = (x, y)
                t = self.editor.current_tool
                if t in ["pencil", "eraser"]:
                    self.editor.apply_brush(x, y, erase=(t == "eraser"))
                elif t == "eyedropper":
                    self.editor.pick_color(x, y)
                elif t == "bucket":
                    self.editor.apply_flood_fill(x, y)
                self.update()

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        z = self.get_zoom()
        x = int(event.position().x() // z)
        y = int(event.position().y() // z)
        w, h = self.editor.get_active_dimensions()

        if 0 <= x < w and 0 <= y < h:
            if event.buttons() & Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
                t = self.editor.current_tool
                if t in ["pencil", "eraser"]:
                    self.editor.apply_brush(x, y, erase=(t == "eraser"))
                elif t in ["line", "rect_stroke", "rect_fill", "ellipse_stroke", "ellipse_fill"]:
                    self.temp_draw_pos = (x, y)
                self.update()

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton and self.drag_start_pos:
            z = self.get_zoom()
            w, h = self.editor.get_active_dimensions()
            x = max(0, min(w - 1, int(event.position().x() // z)))
            y = max(0, min(h - 1, int(event.position().y() // z)))

            t = self.editor.current_tool
            if t == "line": self.editor.apply_line(self.drag_start_pos[0], self.drag_start_pos[1], x, y)
            elif t == "rect_stroke": self.editor.apply_rect(self.drag_start_pos[0], self.drag_start_pos[1], x, y, fill=False)
            elif t == "rect_fill": self.editor.apply_rect(self.drag_start_pos[0], self.drag_start_pos[1], x, y, fill=True)
            elif t == "ellipse_stroke": self.editor.apply_ellipse(self.drag_start_pos[0], self.drag_start_pos[1], x, y, fill=False)
            elif t == "ellipse_fill": self.editor.apply_ellipse(self.drag_start_pos[0], self.drag_start_pos[1], x, y, fill=True)

            self.drag_start_pos = None
            self.temp_draw_pos = None
            self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        w, h = self.editor.get_active_dimensions()
        z = self.get_zoom()

        # 1. Checkerboard pattern
        for y in range(0, self.height(), z):
            for x in range(0, self.width(), z):
                col = QColor(30, 30, 30) if ((x // z) + (y // z)) % 2 == 0 else QColor(45, 45, 45)
                painter.fillRect(x, y, z, z, col)

        # 2. Render Pixels
        for py in range(h):
            for px in range(w):
                c_idx = self.editor.get_pixel_index(px, py)
                rgb = self.editor.get_color_for_index(c_idx)
                if c_idx != 0:
                    painter.fillRect(px * z, py * z, z, z, QColor(*rgb))

        # 3. Shape Previews
        if self.drag_start_pos and self.temp_draw_pos:
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 0, 220), 2))
            x1, y1 = self.drag_start_pos[0] * z, self.drag_start_pos[1] * z
            x2, y2 = self.temp_draw_pos[0] * z, self.temp_draw_pos[1] * z
            t = self.editor.current_tool

            if t == "line": painter.drawLine(x1, y1, x2, y2)
            elif t in ["rect_stroke", "rect_fill"]:
                rx, ry, rw, rh = min(x1, x2), min(y1, y2), abs(x2 - x1) + z, abs(y2 - y1) + z
                if t == "rect_fill": painter.fillRect(rx, ry, rw, rh, QColor(255, 255, 0, 100))
                painter.drawRect(rx, ry, rw, rh)
            elif t in ["ellipse_stroke", "ellipse_fill"]:
                rx, ry, rw, rh = min(x1, x2), min(y1, y2), abs(x2 - x1) + z, abs(y2 - y1) + z
                if t == "ellipse_fill": painter.setBrush(QColor(255, 255, 0, 100))
                painter.drawEllipse(rx, ry, rw, rh)

        # 4. Grids
        if self.editor.show_pixel_grid and z >= 6:
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 255, 40), 1))
            for x in range(0, self.width(), z): painter.drawLine(x, 0, x, self.height())
            for y in range(0, self.height(), z): painter.drawLine(0, y, self.width(), y)

        if self.editor.show_tile_grid:
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 0, 90), 1))
            tz = 8 * z
            for x in range(0, self.width(), tz): painter.drawLine(x, 0, x, self.height())
            for y in range(0, self.height(), tz): painter.drawLine(0, y, self.width(), y)

        painter.end()


class TilesetGridWidget(QWidget):
    """YY-CHR Style Pattern Table with full 32-tile width to fit all VRAM glyphs."""
    def __init__(self, editor, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.editor = editor
        self.tiles_per_row = 32 # 32 tiles per row = 256 pixels wide
        self.zoom = 2
        self.update_dimensions()

    def update_dimensions(self):
        total_tiles = self.editor.get_current_total_tiles()
        rows = max(32, (total_tiles + self.tiles_per_row - 1) // self.tiles_per_row)
        self.setFixedSize(self.tiles_per_row * 8 * self.zoom, rows * 8 * self.zoom)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            tx = int(event.position().x() // (8 * self.zoom))
            ty = int(event.position().y() // (8 * self.zoom))
            if 0 <= tx < self.tiles_per_row:
                tile_idx = ty * self.tiles_per_row + tx
                self.editor.select_tile(tile_idx)
                self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.fillRect(self.rect(), QColor(20, 20, 20))
        img = self.editor.render_tileset_image()
        if img:
            qimg = QImage(img.tobytes(), img.width, img.height, img.width * 4, QImage.Format.Format_RGBA8888)
            scaled = qimg.scaled(self.size(), Qt.AspectRatioMode.IgnoreAspectRatio, Qt.TransformationMode.FastTransformation)
            painter.drawImage(0, 0, scaled)

        st = self.editor.selected_tile_idx
        sx = (st % self.tiles_per_row) * 8 * self.zoom
        sy = (st // self.tiles_per_row) * 8 * self.zoom
        painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 0), 2))
        painter.drawRect(sx, sy, 8 * self.zoom, 8 * self.zoom)
        painter.end()


class GraphicEditorDialog(QDialog):
    SPRITE_SIZES = {
        0: [(8, 8), (16, 16), (32, 32), (64, 64)],
        1: [(16, 8), (32, 8), (32, 16), (64, 32)],
        2: [(8, 16), (8, 32), (16, 32), (32, 64)]
    }

    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.setWindowTitle("In-Game Graphic Editor (YY-CHR)")
        self.resize(1260, 800)

        if self.worker:
            self.was_paused = self.worker.is_paused
            self.worker.is_paused = True

        # Editor State
        self.mode = "tiles"
        self.current_tool = "pencil"
        self.brush_size = 1
        self.selected_color_idx = 1
        self.selected_pal_bank = 0
        self.selected_cbb = 0 # 0-5, or 6 for entire VRAM
        self.is_8bpp = False
        self.selected_tile_idx = 0
        self.selected_sprite_idx = 0

        self.show_tile_grid = True
        self.show_pixel_grid = True

        self.vram_copy = None
        self.original_vram = None
        self.pram_copy = None
        self.oam_copy = None
        self.palette_colors = []
        self.modified_vram_offsets = set()

        self._load_memory()
        self._init_ui()
        self.select_tile(0)

    def _load_memory(self):
        vram = self.worker.get_memory_region("VRAM") if self.worker else None
        self.vram_copy = bytearray(vram) if vram else bytearray(96 * 1024)
        self.original_vram = bytes(self.vram_copy)

        pram = self.worker.get_memory_region("PRAM") if self.worker else None
        self.pram_copy = bytearray(pram) if pram else bytearray(1024)

        oam = self.worker.get_memory_region("OAM") if self.worker else None
        self.oam_copy = bytearray(oam) if oam else bytearray(1024)

        if not all(b == 0 for b in self.pram_copy[:1024]):
            words = struct.unpack("<512H", self.pram_copy[:1024])
            self.palette_colors = [bgr555_to_rgb(w) for w in words]
        else:
            frame = getattr(self.worker, 'latest_video_frame', None)
            if frame is not None:
                flat = frame.reshape(-1, 3)
                unique = np.unique(flat, axis=0)
                self.palette_colors = [(int(c[0]), int(c[1]), int(c[2])) for c in unique[:512]]
            while len(self.palette_colors) < 512: self.palette_colors.append((0, 0, 0))

    def refresh_from_emulator(self):
        """Pulls the latest live VRAM from the emulator while keeping existing window open."""
        self._load_memory()
        self.tileset_grid.update_dimensions()
        self.tileset_grid.update()
        self.zoom_canvas.update()
        self.populate_sprite_table()
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Refreshed", "VRAM, PRAM, and OAM refreshed from live game state.")

        self.cbb_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_cbb_changed)

    def on_bpp_toggled(self, checked: bool):
        self.is_8bpp = checked
        self.tileset_grid.update_dimensions()
        self.tileset_grid.update()
        self.zoom_canvas.update()

    def get_current_total_tiles(self) -> int:
        bpp_mult = 64 if self.is_8bpp else 32
        if self.selected_cbb == 6: # Entire 96 KB VRAM
            return len(self.vram_copy) // bpp_mult
        return 0x4000 // bpp_mult

    def on_tab_changed(self, idx: int):
        self.mode = "tiles" if idx == 0 else "sprites"
        if self.mode == "sprites":
            self.on_sprite_table_selected()
        else:
            self.select_tile(self.selected_tile_idx)

    def set_tool(self, tool_name: str):
        self.current_tool = tool_name
        self.btn_pencil.setChecked(tool_name == "pencil")
        self.btn_eraser.setChecked(tool_name == "eraser")
        self.btn_line.setChecked(tool_name == "line")
        self.btn_picker.setChecked(tool_name == "eyedropper")
        self.btn_bucket.setChecked(tool_name == "bucket")
        self.btn_rect.setChecked(tool_name == "rect_stroke")
        self.btn_fill.setChecked(tool_name == "rect_fill")
        self.btn_ellipse.setChecked(tool_name == "ellipse_stroke")
        self.btn_ellipse_fill.setChecked(tool_name == "ellipse_fill")

    def on_brush_size_changed(self, size: int):
        self.brush_size = size

    def toggle_tile_grid(self, checked):
        self.show_tile_grid = checked
        self.zoom_canvas.update()

    def toggle_pixel_grid(self, checked):
        self.show_pixel_grid = checked
        self.zoom_canvas.update()

    def on_cbb_changed(self, idx: int):
        self.selected_cbb = idx
        self.tileset_grid.update_dimensions()
        self.select_tile(self.selected_tile_idx)
        self.tileset_grid.update()

    def select_tile(self, tile_idx: int):
        max_t = self.get_current_total_tiles()
        self.selected_tile_idx = max(0, min(max_t - 1, tile_idx))
        
        bpp_mult = 64 if self.is_8bpp else 32
        base_offset = 0 if self.selected_cbb == 6 else (self.selected_cbb * 0x4000)
        addr = 0x06000000 + base_offset + (self.selected_tile_idx * bpp_mult)
        
        if hasattr(self, 'info_label') and self.info_label is not None:
            self.info_label.setText(
                f"Mode: {'8bpp' if self.is_8bpp else '4bpp'} Tileset\n"
                f"Tile: 0x{self.selected_tile_idx:03X} ({self.selected_tile_idx})\n"
                f"Addr: 0x{addr:08X}\n"
                f"Bank: {self.selected_pal_bank}"
            )
        if hasattr(self, 'zoom_canvas') and self.zoom_canvas is not None:
            self.zoom_canvas.sizeHint()
            self.zoom_canvas.update()

    def populate_sprite_table(self):
        words = struct.unpack("<512H", self.oam_copy[:1024]) if len(self.oam_copy) >= 1024 else [0]*512
        for i in range(128):
            a0, a1, a2 = words[i*4], words[i*4+1], words[i*4+2]
            y = a0 & 0xFF
            shape = (a0 >> 14) & 0x3
            x = a1 & 0x1FF
            if x >= 240: x -= 512
            size_idx = (a1 >> 14) & 0x3
            dim = self.SPRITE_SIZES.get(shape, [(8, 8)]*4)[size_idx]
            tile = a2 & 0x3FF
            pal = (a2 >> 12) & 0xF
            h_flip = bool(a1 & (1 << 12))

            self.sprite_table.setItem(i, 0, QTableWidgetItem(f"{i:03d}"))
            self.sprite_table.setItem(i, 1, QTableWidgetItem(str(x)))
            self.sprite_table.setItem(i, 2, QTableWidgetItem(str(y)))
            self.sprite_table.setItem(i, 3, QTableWidgetItem(f"{dim[0]}x{dim[1]}"))
            self.sprite_table.setItem(i, 4, QTableWidgetItem(f"0x{tile:03X}"))
            self.sprite_table.setItem(i, 5, QTableWidgetItem(str(pal)))
            self.sprite_table.setItem(i, 6, QTableWidgetItem(f"{'H' if h_flip else '-'}"))

    def on_sprite_table_selected(self):
        rows = self.sprite_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if not rows: return
        self.selected_sprite_idx = rows[0].row()
        
        words = struct.unpack("<512H", self.oam_copy[:1024])
        a0, a1, a2 = words[self.selected_sprite_idx*4], words[self.selected_sprite_idx*4+1], words[self.selected_sprite_idx*4+2]
        shape = (a0 >> 14) & 0x3
        size_idx = (a1 >> 14) & 0x3
        w, h = self.SPRITE_SIZES.get(shape, [(8, 8)]*4)[size_idx]
        start_tile = a2 & 0x3FF
        pal = (a2 >> 12) & 0xF

        self.selected_cbb = 4
        self.selected_tile_idx = start_tile
        self.selected_pal_bank = pal
        self.bank_spin.blockSignals(True)
        self.bank_spin.setValue(pal)
        self.bank_spin.blockSignals(False)
        self._build_swatches()

        addr = 0x06010000 + (start_tile * 32)
        if hasattr(self, 'info_label') and self.info_label is not None:
            self.info_label.setText(
                f"Mode: Sprite #{self.selected_sprite_idx:03d}\n"
                f"Size: {w}x{h}\n"
                f"Base Tile: 0x{start_tile:03X}\n"
                f"Addr: 0x{addr:08X}\n"
                f"Bank: {pal}"
            )
        if hasattr(self, 'zoom_canvas') and self.zoom_canvas is not None:
            self.zoom_canvas.sizeHint()
            self.zoom_canvas.update()

    def get_active_dimensions(self) -> tuple:
        if self.mode == "sprites":
            words = struct.unpack("<512H", self.oam_copy[:1024])
            a0, a1 = words[self.selected_sprite_idx*4], words[self.selected_sprite_idx*4+1]
            shape = (a0 >> 14) & 0x3
            size_idx = (a1 >> 14) & 0x3
            return self.SPRITE_SIZES.get(shape, [(8, 8)]*4)[size_idx]
        return (8, 8)

    def get_pixel_index(self, px: int, py: int) -> int:
        bpp_mult = 64 if self.is_8bpp else 32
        base_offset = 0 if self.selected_cbb == 6 else (self.selected_cbb * 0x4000)

        if self.mode == "sprites":
            tx = px // 8
            ty = py // 8
            lx = px % 8
            ly = py % 8
            tile_offset_idx = self.selected_tile_idx + (ty * 32) + tx
            offset = 0x10000 + (tile_offset_idx * 32) + (ly * 4) + (lx // 2)
            if offset < len(self.vram_copy):
                b = self.vram_copy[offset]
                return (b & 0xF) if (lx % 2 == 0) else ((b >> 4) & 0xF)
        else:
            if not self.is_8bpp:
                offset = base_offset + (self.selected_tile_idx * 32) + (py * 4) + (px // 2)
                if offset < len(self.vram_copy):
                    b = self.vram_copy[offset]
                    return (b & 0xF) if (px % 2 == 0) else ((b >> 4) & 0xF)
            else:
                offset = base_offset + (self.selected_tile_idx * 64) + (py * 8) + px
                if offset < len(self.vram_copy):
                    return self.vram_copy[offset]
        return 0

    def set_pixel_index(self, px: int, py: int, c_idx: int):
        bpp_mult = 64 if self.is_8bpp else 32
        base_offset = 0 if self.selected_cbb == 6 else (self.selected_cbb * 0x4000)

        if self.mode == "sprites":
            tx = px // 8
            ty = py // 8
            lx = px % 8
            ly = py % 8
            tile_offset_idx = self.selected_tile_idx + (ty * 32) + tx
            offset = 0x10000 + (tile_offset_idx * 32) + (ly * 4) + (lx // 2)
            if offset < len(self.vram_copy):
                old = self.vram_copy[offset]
                new = (old & 0xF0) | (c_idx & 0x0F) if (lx % 2 == 0) else (old & 0x0F) | ((c_idx & 0x0F) << 4)
                self.vram_copy[offset] = new
                self.modified_vram_offsets.add(offset)
                if self.worker: self.worker.write_memory_region("VRAM", offset, bytes([new]))
        else:
            if not self.is_8bpp:
                offset = base_offset + (self.selected_tile_idx * 32) + (py * 4) + (px // 2)
                if offset < len(self.vram_copy):
                    old = self.vram_copy[offset]
                    new = (old & 0xF0) | (c_idx & 0x0F) if (px % 2 == 0) else (old & 0x0F) | ((c_idx & 0x0F) << 4)
                    self.vram_copy[offset] = new
                    self.modified_vram_offsets.add(offset)
                    if self.worker: self.worker.write_memory_region("VRAM", offset, bytes([new]))
            else:
                offset = base_offset + (self.selected_tile_idx * 64) + (py * 8) + px
                if offset < len(self.vram_copy):
                    self.vram_copy[offset] = c_idx & 0xFF
                    self.modified_vram_offsets.add(offset)
                    if self.worker: self.worker.write_memory_region("VRAM", offset, bytes([c_idx & 0xFF]))

    def get_color_for_index(self, c_idx: int) -> tuple:
        if self.is_8bpp:
            return self.palette_colors[c_idx % len(self.palette_colors)]
        is_obj = (self.selected_cbb in [4, 5] or self.mode == "sprites")
        flat_idx = (self.selected_pal_bank * 16 + c_idx)
        if is_obj: flat_idx += 256
        return self.palette_colors[flat_idx % len(self.palette_colors)]

    def on_pal_bank_changed(self, bank: int):
        self.selected_pal_bank = bank
        self._build_swatches()
        if hasattr(self, 'zoom_canvas') and self.zoom_canvas is not None: self.zoom_canvas.update()
        if hasattr(self, 'tileset_grid') and self.tileset_grid is not None: self.tileset_grid.update()

    def _build_swatches(self):
        for i in reversed(range(self.swatch_layout.count())):
            w = self.swatch_layout.itemAt(i).widget()
            if w: w.setParent(None)

        for c_idx in range(16):
            rgb = self.get_color_for_index(c_idx)
            btn = QPushButton()
            btn.setFixedSize(14, 22)
            border = "2px solid #FFF" if c_idx == self.selected_color_idx else "1px solid #333"
            btn.setStyleSheet(f"background-color: rgb({rgb[0]},{rgb[1]},{rgb[2]}); border: {border};")
            btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, ci=c_idx: self.select_color_index(ci))
            self.swatch_layout.addWidget(btn)

    def select_color_index(self, idx: int):
        self.selected_color_idx = idx
        self._build_swatches()

    def open_rgb_color_picker(self):
        current_rgb = self.get_color_for_index(self.selected_color_idx)
        chosen = QColorDialog.getColor(QColor(*current_rgb), self, "Edit Active Palette Color")
        if chosen.isValid():
            new_rgb = (int(chosen.red()), int(chosen.green()), int(chosen.blue()))
            is_obj = (self.selected_cbb in [4, 5] or self.mode == "sprites")
            flat_idx = (self.selected_pal_bank * 16 + self.selected_color_idx)
            if is_obj: flat_idx += 256
            self.palette_colors[flat_idx % len(self.palette_colors)] = new_rgb
            
            bgr555 = rgb_to_bgr555(*new_rgb)
            pram_offset = flat_idx * 2

            if self.pram_copy and pram_offset + 2 <= len(self.pram_copy):
                self.pram_copy[pram_offset:pram_offset+2] = bgr555.to_bytes(2, "little")

            if self.worker:
                self.worker.write_memory_region("PRAM", pram_offset, bgr555.to_bytes(2, "little"))

            self._build_swatches()
            self.zoom_canvas.update()
            self.tileset_grid.update()

    def render_tileset_image(self) -> Image.Image:
        bpp_mult = 64 if self.is_8bpp else 32
        base_offset = 0 if self.selected_cbb == 6 else (self.selected_cbb * 0x4000)
        total_tiles = self.get_current_total_tiles()
        
        tiles_per_row = 32
        rows = max(32, (total_tiles + tiles_per_row - 1) // tiles_per_row)

        img = Image.new("RGBA", (tiles_per_row * 8, rows * 8), (20, 20, 20, 255))
        for t in range(total_tiles):
            tx = (t % tiles_per_row) * 8
            ty = (t // tiles_per_row) * 8
            t_offset = base_offset + (t * bpp_mult)
            if t_offset + bpp_mult > len(self.vram_copy): break
            t_data = self.vram_copy[t_offset:t_offset + bpp_mult]

            for py in range(8):
                for px in range(8):
                    if not self.is_8bpp:
                        b = t_data[py * 4 + px // 2]
                        c_idx = (b & 0xF) if (px % 2 == 0) else ((b >> 4) & 0xF)
                    else:
                        c_idx = t_data[py * 8 + px]
                    rgb = self.get_color_for_index(c_idx)
                    img.putpixel((tx + px, ty + py), (*rgb, 255))
        return img

    # ==========================================================================
    # Drawing & Pixel Editing Operations
    # ==========================================================================
    def apply_brush(self, center_x: int, center_y: int, erase: bool = False):
        color_val = 0 if erase else self.selected_color_idx
        r = self.brush_size // 2
        w, h = self.get_active_dimensions()

        for dy in range(-r, self.brush_size - r):
            for dx in range(-r, self.brush_size - r):
                nx, ny = center_x + dx, center_y + dy
                if 0 <= nx < w and 0 <= ny < h:
                    self.set_pixel_index(nx, ny, color_val)

        self.zoom_canvas.update()
        self.tileset_grid.update()

    def pick_color(self, x: int, y: int):
        c_idx = self.get_pixel_index(x, y)
        self.select_color_index(c_idx)
        self.set_tool("pencil")

    def apply_flood_fill(self, start_x: int, start_y: int):
        target_idx = self.get_pixel_index(start_x, start_y)
        rep_idx = self.selected_color_idx
        if target_idx == rep_idx: return

        w, h = self.get_active_dimensions()
        queue = deque([(start_x, start_y)])
        visited = set([(start_x, start_y)])

        while queue:
            cx, cy = queue.popleft()
            if self.get_pixel_index(cx, cy) == target_idx:
                self.set_pixel_index(cx, cy, rep_idx)
                for nx, ny in [(cx+1, cy), (cx-1, cy), (cx, cy+1), (cx, cy-1)]:
                    if 0 <= nx < w and 0 <= ny < h and (nx, ny) not in visited:
                        visited.add((nx, ny))
                        queue.append((nx, ny))

        self.zoom_canvas.update()
        self.tileset_grid.update()

    def apply_line(self, x0: int, y0: int, x1: int, y1: int):
        dx, dy = abs(x1 - x0), abs(y1 - y0)
        sx = 1 if x0 < x1 else -1
        sy = 1 if y0 < y1 else -1
        err = dx - dy
        while True:
            self.apply_brush(x0, y0)
            if x0 == x1 and y0 == y1: break
            e2 = 2 * err
            if e2 > -dy: err -= dy; x0 += sx
            if e2 < dx: err += dx; y0 += sy

    def apply_rect(self, x0: int, y0: int, x1: int, y1: int, fill: bool = False):
        rx, ry = min(x0, x1), min(y0, y1)
        rw, rh = abs(x1 - x0), abs(y1 - y0)
        if fill:
            for y in range(ry, ry + rh + 1):
                for x in range(rx, rx + rw + 1): self.apply_brush(x, y)
        else:
            for x in range(rx, rx + rw + 1):
                self.apply_brush(x, ry)
                self.apply_brush(x, ry + rh)
            for y in range(ry, ry + rh + 1):
                self.apply_brush(rx, y)
                self.apply_brush(rx + rw, y)

    def apply_ellipse(self, x0: int, y0: int, x1: int, y1: int, fill: bool = False):
        rx, ry = min(x0, x1), min(y0, y1)
        w, h = abs(x1 - x0), abs(y1 - y0)
        if w == 0 or h == 0: return

        cx, cy = rx + w / 2, ry + h / 2
        rad_x, rad_y = w / 2, h / 2

        for y in range(ry, ry + h + 1):
            for x in range(rx, rx + w + 1):
                norm = ((x - cx) / rad_x)**2 + ((y - cy) / rad_y)**2
                if fill and norm <= 1.0:
                    self.apply_brush(x, y)
                elif not fill and abs(norm - 1.0) <= 0.15:
                    self.apply_brush(x, y)

    # ==========================================================================
    # ROM Saving & Advanced Patch Generation
    # ==========================================================================
    def get_patched_rom_bytes(self) -> tuple:
        if not self.worker or not self.worker.rom_path or not os.path.exists(self.worker.rom_path):
            return None, None, 0

        with open(self.worker.rom_path, "rb") as f:
            rom_data = bytearray(f.read())

        orig_rom = bytes(rom_data)
        matches_found = 0
        bpp_mult = 64 if self.is_8bpp else 32
        modified_tile_addresses = {off - (off % bpp_mult) for off in self.modified_vram_offsets}

        for tile_addr in modified_tile_addresses:
            orig_tile = self.original_vram[tile_addr:tile_addr + bpp_mult]
            new_tile = bytes(self.vram_copy[tile_addr:tile_addr + bpp_mult])
            if orig_tile == new_tile: continue

            pos = 0
            while True:
                found = rom_data.find(orig_tile, pos)
                if found == -1: break
                rom_data[found:found + bpp_mult] = new_tile
                matches_found += 1
                pos = found + bpp_mult

        if matches_found == 0 and modified_tile_addresses:
            for offset in range(0, len(rom_data) - 4, 4):
                if rom_data[offset] == 0x10:
                    decomp, comp_len = lz77_decompress(rom_data, offset)
                    if decomp and comp_len > 4:
                        decomp_arr = bytearray(decomp)
                        stream_modified = False

                        for tile_addr in modified_tile_addresses:
                            orig_tile = self.original_vram[tile_addr:tile_addr + bpp_mult]
                            new_tile = bytes(self.vram_copy[tile_addr:tile_addr + bpp_mult])
                            
                            t_pos = decomp_arr.find(orig_tile)
                            if t_pos != -1:
                                decomp_arr[t_pos:t_pos + bpp_mult] = new_tile
                                stream_modified = True
                                matches_found += 1

                        if stream_modified:
                            recomp = lz77_compress(bytes(decomp_arr))
                            if len(recomp) <= comp_len:
                                rom_data[offset:offset + len(recomp)] = recomp
                                rom_data[offset + len(recomp):offset + comp_len] = bytes(comp_len - len(recomp))

        return bytes(rom_data), orig_rom, matches_found

    def save_modified_rom(self):
        result = self.get_patched_rom_bytes()
        if not result or not result[0]:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "ROM Error", "Could not locate original game ROM on disk.")
            return

        patched_rom, _, matches = result
        if matches == 0:
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self, "Notice", 
                "No matching tile patterns were found in the ROM file.\n"
                "(The graphics may be decompressed dynamically into RAM).\n\n"
                "Tip: Use 'Save Raw VRAM Dump (.bin)' to export your modified graphics directly!"
            )
            return

        default_name = f"patched_{os.path.basename(self.worker.rom_path)}"
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Save Modified ROM", default_name, "GBA ROMs (*.gba);;All Files (*.*)")
        if filepath:
            with open(filepath, "wb") as f: f.write(patched_rom)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"Patched {matches} tile instance(s) into:\n{filepath}")

    def export_ips_patch(self):
        result = self.get_patched_rom_bytes()
        if not result or not result[0]:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "ROM Error", "Could not locate original ROM file.")
            return

        patched_rom, orig_rom, matches = result
        if matches == 0:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Notice", "No matched tile changes to generate an IPS patch from.")
            return

        ips_data = create_ips_patch(orig_rom, patched_rom)
        default_name = f"{os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(self.worker.rom_path))[0]}.ips"
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export IPS Patch", default_name, "IPS Patches (*.ips)")
        if filepath:
            with open(filepath, "wb") as f: f.write(ips_data)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Patch Created", f"Successfully created IPS Patch ({len(ips_data)} bytes) at:\n{filepath}")

    def export_raw_vram_dump(self):
        default_name = f"vram_dump_{datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.bin"
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Save Raw VRAM Dump", default_name, "Binary Files (*.bin)")
        if filepath:
            with open(filepath, "wb") as f: f.write(self.vram_copy)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Saved", f"Exported 96 KB VRAM buffer to:\n{filepath}")

    def export_current_to_png(self):
        w, h = self.get_active_dimensions()
        img = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        for py in range(h):
            for px in range(w):
                c_idx = self.get_pixel_index(px, py)
                if c_idx != 0:
                    rgb = self.get_color_for_index(c_idx)
                    img.putpixel((px, py), (*rgb, 255))
        
        default_name = f"export_{self.mode}_{self.selected_tile_idx}.png"
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export Graphic to PNG", default_name, "PNG Images (*.png)")
        if filepath:
            img.save(filepath)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Exported", f"Saved to:\n{filepath}")

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        """Flushes the full modified VRAM buffer to the live core and unpauses."""
        if self.worker and self.vram_copy:
            self.worker.write_memory_region("VRAM", 0, bytes(self.vram_copy))
        if self.worker and hasattr(self, 'was_paused'):
            self.worker.is_paused = self.was_paused
        event.accept()

--- END OF FILE graphic_editor.py ---

# gui.py
import os
import sys
import html
import math
import datetime
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QMainWindow, QWidget, QFileDialog, QMessageBox, 
                             QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
                             QComboBox, QPushButton, QTextEdit, QSplitter, QFrame)
from PyQt6.QtGui import (QImage, QPainter, QAction, QActionGroup, QKeySequence, 
                         QColor, QFont, QPen, QBrush)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, QPoint, QRect, QEvent

from core_worker import EmulatorWorker
from controller import ControllerManager, ControllerConfigDialog, LIBRETRO_BTNS, QT_KEY_MAP
from dev_tools import (PaletteViewerDialog, SpriteViewerDialog, TileViewerDialog, 
                       MapViewerDialog, FrameInspectorDialog, MemoryViewerDialog, 
                       MemorySearchDialog, IORegisterViewerDialog)

class VideoWidget(QWidget):
    """High-speed rendering surface for emulator video with pixel ruler support."""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_OpaquePaintEvent)
        self.setMinimumSize(240, 160)
        self.setMouseTracking(True) 
        self.setFocusPolicy(Qt.FocusPolicy.StrongFocus)
        self.current_frame_data = None
        self.qimage = None
        
        # OSD Setup
        self.osd_text = ""
        self.osd_timer = QTimer()
        self.osd_timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self.osd_timer.timeout.connect(self.clear_osd)

        # Pixel Ruler State
        self.ruler_enabled = False
        self.hover_pos = None
        self.ruler_start = None
        self.ruler_end = None
        self.is_dragging = False

        # Mouse Gyro State
        self.mouse_gyro_active = False
        self.gyro_drag_center = None

    def set_frame(self, frame_data: np.ndarray):
        self.current_frame_data = frame_data
        height, width, channel = frame_data.shape
        bytes_per_line = 3 * width
        self.qimage = QImage(self.current_frame_data.data, width, height, 
                             bytes_per_line, QImage.Format.Format_RGB888)
        self.update()

    def show_osd(self, text: str, duration_ms: int = 2000):
        self.osd_text = text
        self.osd_timer.start(duration_ms)
        self.update()

    def clear_osd(self):
        self.osd_text = ""
        self.update()

    def get_gba_coords(self, pos) -> tuple:
        if not self.qimage:
            return 0, 0
        widget_w, widget_h = self.width(), self.height()
        img_w, img_h = self.qimage.width(), self.qimage.height()
        
        scale = min(widget_w / img_w, widget_h / img_h)
        offset_x = (widget_w - (img_w * scale)) / 2
        offset_y = (widget_h - (img_h * scale)) / 2
        
        gba_x = int((pos.x() - offset_x) / scale)
        gba_y = int((pos.y() - offset_y) / scale)
        
        return max(0, min(img_w - 1, gba_x)), max(0, min(img_h - 1, gba_y))

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        self.setFocus()

        # Mouse Gyro Twist Gesture (Right-Click or Middle-Click drag)
        if event.button() in [Qt.MouseButton.RightButton, Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton]:
            parent_win = self.window()
            if hasattr(parent_win, 'config') and parent_win.config.get("sensors", "mouse_gyro"):
                self.mouse_gyro_active = True
                self.gyro_drag_center = QPoint(self.width() // 2, self.height() // 2)
                self._update_mouse_gyro(event.position().toPoint())
                self.update()
                return

        # Pixel Ruler Drag
        if self.ruler_enabled and event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            self.ruler_start = self.get_gba_coords(event.position())
            self.ruler_end = self.ruler_start
            self.is_dragging = True
            self.update()

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self.mouse_gyro_active:
            self._update_mouse_gyro(event.position().toPoint())
            self.update()
            return

        if self.ruler_enabled:
            self.hover_pos = self.get_gba_coords(event.position())
            if self.is_dragging and self.ruler_start:
                self.ruler_end = self.hover_pos
            self.update()

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() in [Qt.MouseButton.RightButton, Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton] and self.mouse_gyro_active:
            self.mouse_gyro_active = False
            self.update()
            return

        if self.ruler_enabled and event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            self.is_dragging = False
            self.update()

    def _update_mouse_gyro(self, pos: QPoint):
        if not self.gyro_drag_center: return
        dx = pos.x() - self.gyro_drag_center.x()
        dy = pos.y() - self.gyro_drag_center.y()
        if dx == 0 and dy == 0: return

        angle = math.degrees(math.atan2(dx, -dy))
        parent_win = self.window()
        if hasattr(parent_win, 'worker') and parent_win.worker:
            parent_win.worker.set_gyro_angle(angle)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.fillRect(self.rect(), QColor(0, 0, 0))

        if self.qimage:
            scaled_img = self.qimage.scaled(self.rect().size(), 
                                            Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio, 
                                            Qt.TransformationMode.FastTransformation)
            x = (self.width() - scaled_img.width()) // 2
            y = (self.height() - scaled_img.height()) // 2
            painter.drawImage(x, y, scaled_img)

            img_w, img_h = self.qimage.width(), self.qimage.height()
            scale = min(self.width() / img_w, self.height() / img_h)
            ox = (self.width() - img_w * scale) / 2
            oy = (self.height() - img_h * scale) / 2

            # Pixel Ruler Overlay
            if self.ruler_enabled:
                if self.hover_pos:
                    hx, hy = self.hover_pos
                    sx, sy = ox + hx * scale, oy + hy * scale
                    painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(0, 255, 255, 150), 1))
                    painter.drawLine(int(ox), int(sy), int(ox + img_w * scale), int(sy))
                    painter.drawLine(int(sx), int(oy), int(sx), int(oy + img_h * scale))
                    
                    painter.setPen(QColor(0, 255, 255))
                    painter.setFont(QFont("Arial", 10, QFont.Weight.Bold))
                    painter.drawText(int(ox) + 5, int(oy) + 15, f"Cursor: {hx}, {hy}")

                if self.ruler_start and self.ruler_end:
                    x1, y1 = self.ruler_start
                    x2, y2 = self.ruler_end
                    rx, ry = min(x1, x2), min(y1, y2)
                    rw, rh = abs(x2 - x1) + 1, abs(y2 - y1) + 1

                    screen_x = ox + rx * scale
                    screen_y = oy + ry * scale
                    screen_w = rw * scale
                    screen_h = rh * scale

                    painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 0, 255), 2))
                    painter.setBrush(QColor(255, 0, 255, 60))
                    painter.drawRect(int(screen_x), int(screen_y), int(screen_w), int(screen_h))

                    label = f" W: {rw}px \n H: {rh}px "
                    painter.setPen(QColor(255, 255, 255))
                    painter.setFont(QFont("Arial", 11, QFont.Weight.Bold))
                    tr = painter.fontMetrics().boundingRect(label)
                    tr.moveTo(int(screen_x), int(screen_y) - tr.height())
                    painter.fillRect(tr, QColor(0, 0, 0, 180))
                    painter.drawText(tr, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft, label)

        # Global OSD Message (Top-Right)
        if self.osd_text:
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(0, 255, 0)))
            painter.setFont(QFont("Arial", 15, QFont.Weight.Bold))
            painter.drawText(self.rect().adjusted(10, 10, -10, -10), 
                             Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight, 
                             self.osd_text)
        painter.end()


class SensorHudWidget(QFrame):
    """Separated status bar situated below the video widget and above the translation panel."""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.StyledPanel)
        self.setFixedHeight(34)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            SensorHudWidget {
                background-color: #12151D;
                border-top: 1px solid #232938;
                border-bottom: 1px solid #232938;
            }
            QLabel {
                font-family: 'Cascadia Code', 'Consolas', 'Courier New', monospace;
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: bold;
            }
        """)

        self.current_solar_level = 5
        self.rumble_active = False
        self.rumble_strength = 0.0

        self._rumble_off_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._rumble_off_timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self._rumble_off_timer.timeout.connect(self._clear_rumble)

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(12, 0, 12, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(18)

        self.lbl_game = QLabel("🎮 Game: [----] Unknown")
        self.lbl_game.setStyleSheet("color: #90CAF9;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_game)

        self.lbl_solar = QLabel("☀️ Solar: [■■■■■□□□□□] 5/10")
        self.lbl_solar.setStyleSheet("color: #FFD54F;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_solar)

        self.lbl_rumble = QLabel("📳 Rumble: [□□□□□] OFF")
        self.lbl_rumble.setStyleSheet("color: #78909C;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_rumble)

        self.lbl_gyro = QLabel("🎯 Gyro: [─────●─────] 0°")
        self.lbl_gyro.setStyleSheet("color: #4DD0E1;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_gyro)

        layout.addStretch()

    def on_game_identified(self, title: str, code: str, hw_type: str):
        type_str = f" ({hw_type.replace('_', ' ').title()})" if hw_type != "None" else ""
        self.lbl_game.setText(f"🎮 Game: [{code}] {title}{type_str}")

    def on_solar_updated(self, level: int):
        self.current_solar_level = level
        sun_filled = "■" * level
        sun_empty = "□" * (10 - level)
        self.lbl_solar.setText(f"☀️ Solar: [{sun_filled}{sun_empty}] {level}/10")

    def on_rumble_triggered(self, low_freq: float, high_freq: float, duration_ms: int):
        if low_freq <= 0.0 and high_freq <= 0.0:
            self._clear_rumble()
            return

        self.rumble_active = True
        self.rumble_strength = max(low_freq, high_freq)
        pct = int(self.rumble_strength * 100)
        filled = max(1, min(5, int(self.rumble_strength * 5)))
        bars = "■" * filled + "□" * (5 - filled)
        self.lbl_rumble.setText(f"📳 Rumble: [{bars}] ACTIVE ({pct}%)")
        self.lbl_rumble.setStyleSheet("color: #FF5252;")
        self._rumble_off_timer.start(max(90, duration_ms))

    def _clear_rumble(self):
        self.rumble_active = False
        self.rumble_strength = 0.0
        self.lbl_rumble.setText("📳 Rumble: [□□□□□] OFF")
        self.lbl_rumble.setStyleSheet("color: #78909C;")

    def on_tilt_updated(self, x: float, y: float, angle: float):
        deg = int(angle) % 360
        idx = max(0, min(10, int((x + 1.0) * 5.0)))
        meter = list("─────●─────")
        meter[idx] = "●"
        meter_str = "".join(meter)
        self.lbl_gyro.setText(f"🎯 Gyro: [{meter_str}] {deg:3d}° (X:{x:+.2f})")


class AITranslationConfigDialog(QDialog):
    LANGS = [
        ("Japanese", "ja"),
        ("English", "en"),
        ("Vietnamese", "vi")
    ]

    def __init__(self, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.config = config
        self.setWindowTitle("Configure AI Translation")
        self.resize(380, 200)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        row_src = QHBoxLayout()
        row_src.addWidget(QLabel("Source Language (Original):"))
        self.src_combo = QComboBox()
        for label, code in self.LANGS:
            self.src_combo.addItem(label, code)
        row_src.addWidget(self.src_combo)
        layout.addLayout(row_src)

        row_tgt = QHBoxLayout()
        row_tgt.addWidget(QLabel("Target Language (Translate to):"))
        self.tgt_combo = QComboBox()
        for label, code in self.LANGS:
            self.tgt_combo.addItem(label, code)
        row_tgt.addWidget(self.tgt_combo)
        layout.addLayout(row_tgt)

        curr_src = self.config.get("ai_translation", "source_lang") or "ja"
        curr_tgt = self.config.get("ai_translation", "target_lang") or "vi"

        idx_src = self.src_combo.findData(curr_src)
        if idx_src >= 0:
            self.src_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx_src)

        idx_tgt = self.tgt_combo.findData(curr_tgt)
        if idx_tgt >= 0:
            self.tgt_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx_tgt)

        layout.addStretch()

        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_save = QPushButton("Save Settings")
        btn_save.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4CAF50; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 6px;")
        btn_save.clicked.connect(self.save_and_close)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_save)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("Cancel")
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

    def save_and_close(self):
        self.config.set("ai_translation", "source_lang", self.src_combo.currentData())
        self.config.set("ai_translation", "target_lang", self.tgt_combo.currentData())
        self.config.save()
        self.accept()


class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    if sys.platform.startswith('win'):
        DEFAULT_CORE = "mgba_libretro.dll"
    elif sys.platform.startswith('linux'):
        DEFAULT_CORE = "mgba_libretro.so"
    else:
        DEFAULT_CORE = "mgba_libretro.dylib"

    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.worker = None
        self.current_rom = None
        self.scale_factor = 3
        self.current_slot = self.config.get("emulation", "state_slot") or 1
        self.active_tool_window = None 
        self.trans_worker = None
        self._auto_paused = False
        
        self.setWindowTitle("mGBA (PyQt6 Replica)")
        
        script_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
        self.core_path = os.path.join(script_dir, self.DEFAULT_CORE)

        self.controller_manager = ControllerManager(self.config)
        self.gamepad_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.gamepad_timer.timeout.connect(self.poll_gamepad)
        self.gamepad_timer.start(8)

        # 3-Row Vertical Splitter: Game Canvas -> Separated HUD Panel -> Subtitle Panel
        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Orientation.Vertical)
        splitter.setHandleWidth(3)
        splitter.setStyleSheet("QSplitter::handle { background-color: #282828; }")

        # 1. Game Screen (Clean, no HUD overlay)
        self.video_widget = VideoWidget()
        splitter.addWidget(self.video_widget)

        # 2. Separated Sensor & Rumble HUD Panel (OFF by default)
        self.sensor_hud = SensorHudWidget()
        show_hud_pref = bool(self.config.get("sensors", "show_hud", False))
        self.sensor_hud.setVisible(show_hud_pref)
        splitter.addWidget(self.sensor_hud)

        # 3. Translation Panel
        self.translation_panel = QTextEdit()
