        self.status_label = QLabel("Ready.")
        self.status_label.setStyleSheet("font-family: monospace; font-size: 11px; color: #90A4AE; padding: 2px;")
        main_layout.addWidget(self.status_label)

    # =========================================================================
    # Region Switching & Live Watching
    # =========================================================================
    def on_region_changed(self):
        reg_str = self.region_combo.currentText()
        reg_name, base_addr, _ = GBA_REGIONS[reg_str]
        self.current_region_name = reg_name
        self.current_base_addr = base_addr
        self.jump_edit.setText(f"0x{base_addr:08X}")
        self._load_active_region()

    def _load_active_region(self):
        if not self.worker: return
        raw_mem = self.worker.get_memory_region(self.current_region_name)
        if raw_mem is not None:
            self.hex_canvas.set_buffer(raw_mem, self.current_base_addr)
            self.status_label.setText(f"Loaded {self.current_region_name}: {len(raw_mem):,} bytes.")
        else:
            self.hex_canvas.set_buffer(b"", self.current_base_addr)
            self.status_label.setText(f"{self.current_region_name} not available in active core.")
        self.update_status_bar()

    def toggle_live_watch(self, enabled: bool):
        if enabled:
            interval = self.spin_poll.value()
            self.live_timer.start(interval)
            self.status_label.setText(f"⚡ Live Watch Active ({interval} ms). Changed bytes are highlighted.")
        else:
            self.live_timer.stop()
            self.hex_canvas.live_diff_offsets.clear()
            self.hex_canvas.viewport().update()
            self.status_label.setText("Live watch stopped.")

    def poll_live_memory(self):
        if not self.worker: return
        raw = self.worker.get_memory_region(self.current_region_name)
        if raw:
            self.hex_canvas.update_live_buffer(raw)

    # =========================================================================
    # Navigation & Actions
    # =========================================================================
    def on_jump_click(self):
        try:
            val = int(self.jump_edit.text(), 16)
            self.jump_to_address(val)
        except ValueError:
            pass

    def jump_to_address(self, abs_addr: int):
        for reg_key, (reg_name, base, size) in GBA_REGIONS.items():
            if base <= abs_addr < base + size:
                if self.current_region_name != reg_name:
                    idx = self.region_combo.findText(reg_key)
                    if idx >= 0:
                        self.region_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
                offset = abs_addr - base
                self.hex_canvas.set_cursor(offset)
                return

        offset = abs_addr - self.current_base_addr
        self.hex_canvas.set_cursor(offset)

    def on_cursor_moved(self, offset: int):
        self.inspector.update_values(self.hex_canvas.data, offset, self.current_base_addr)
        self.update_status_bar()

    def update_status_bar(self):
        off = self.hex_canvas.cursor_pos
        abs_addr = self.current_base_addr + off
        mod_count = len(self.hex_canvas.modified_map)
        s, e = self.hex_canvas.get_selection_range()
        sel_size = (e - s + 1) if (self.hex_canvas.selection_start is not None and self.hex_canvas.selection_start != self.hex_canvas.selection_end) else 1

        self.status_label.setText(
            f"Address: 0x{abs_addr:08X} (Offset: 0x{off:06X}) | "
            f"Selected: {sel_size} byte(s) | "
            f"Modified: {mod_count} byte(s) | "
            f"Total Size: {len(self.hex_canvas.data):,} bytes"
        )

    # =========================================================================
    # Memory Writing & Exporting
    # =========================================================================
    def commit_changes(self):
        """Flushes all modified bytes directly into active emulator RAM or ROM."""
        if not self.worker or not self.hex_canvas.modified_map:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Notice", "No modifications to commit.")
            return

        if self.current_region_name == "ROM":
            reply = QMessageBox.question(
                self, "Save Modified ROM", 
                "Save changes directly to a new .gba file on disk?",
                QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes | QMessageBox.StandardButton.No
            )
            if reply == QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
                self.export_binary_file()
            return

        success_count = 0
        for off, _ in self.hex_canvas.modified_map.items():
            b = bytes([self.hex_canvas.data[off]])
            if self.worker.write_memory_region(self.current_region_name, off, b):
                success_count += 1

        self.hex_canvas.modified_map.clear()
        self.hex_canvas.viewport().update()
        self.update_status_bar()
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"Committed {success_count} modified byte(s) to {self.current_region_name}!")

    def export_binary_file(self):
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export Region to Binary", f"{self.current_region_name.lower()}_dump.bin", "Binary Files (*.bin *.gba)")
        if filepath:
            with open(filepath, "wb") as f:
                f.write(self.hex_canvas.data)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Exported", f"Saved binary file to:\n{filepath}")

    # =========================================================================
    # Search Engine
    # =========================================================================
    def _get_search_pattern(self) -> bytes:
        query = self.search_edit.text().strip()
        if not query: return None

        if self.cb_search_text.isChecked():
            return query.encode('latin-1', errors='ignore')
        else:
            clean = query.replace(" ", "").replace(",", "").replace("0x", "")
            try:
                return bytes.fromhex(clean)
            except ValueError:
                return None

    def find_next(self):
        pat = self._get_search_pattern()
        if not pat: return

        data = bytes(self.hex_canvas.data)
        start_pos = self.hex_canvas.cursor_pos + 1
        pos = data.find(pat, start_pos)
        if pos == -1:
            pos = data.find(pat, 0)  # Wrap around

        if pos != -1:
            self.hex_canvas.set_cursor(pos)
            self.hex_canvas.selection_start = pos
            self.hex_canvas.selection_end = pos + len(pat) - 1
            self.hex_canvas.viewport().update()
        else:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Not Found", "Pattern not found.")

    def find_prev(self):
        pat = self._get_search_pattern()
        if not pat: return

        data = bytes(self.hex_canvas.data)
        pos = data.rfind(pat, 0, max(0, self.hex_canvas.cursor_pos))
        if pos == -1:
            pos = data.rfind(pat)  # Wrap around

        if pos != -1:
            self.hex_canvas.set_cursor(pos)
            self.hex_canvas.selection_start = pos
            self.hex_canvas.selection_end = pos + len(pat) - 1
            self.hex_canvas.viewport().update()
        else:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Not Found", "Pattern not found.")

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if hasattr(self, 'live_timer'):
            self.live_timer.stop()
        event.accept()

# main.py

# memory_tools.py
import os
import json
import datetime
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QMainWindow, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QPushButton, 
                             QScrollArea, QLabel, QFileDialog, QMessageBox)
from PyQt6.QtGui import QImage, QPixmap
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

# Attempt to load the Enum for Video RAM, safely fallback to hardcoded Libretro IDs
try:
    from pdretro import MemoryRegion
    VRAM_ID = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'VIDEO_RAM', 3)
except ImportError:
    VRAM_ID = 3
    
# Hardcode Palette RAM to 4 (specific to mGBA libretro core mapping)
PRAM_ID = 4 

class MemoryToolsWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        
        self.setWindowTitle("VRAM Tileset & Sprite Viewer")
        self.resize(560, 800)
        
        self._init_ui()
        self._load_vram_preview()

    def _init_ui(self):
        central_widget = QWidget()
        self.setCentralWidget(central_widget)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(central_widget)

        # Export Button (Bright Green for visibility)
        self.export_btn = QPushButton("Export JSON Map & Color/Raw PNGs")
        self.export_btn.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px; background-color: #4CAF50; color: white; padding: 10px;")
        self.export_btn.clicked.connect(self.export_vram_data)
        layout.addWidget(self.export_btn)

        # Scrollable Area for the massive tileset image
        self.scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.scroll_area.setStyleSheet("background-color: black;")
        layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area)

        # Image Label that goes inside the scroll area
        self.image_label = QLabel()
        self.image_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)
        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.image_label)

    def _load_vram_preview(self):
        """Fetches the VRAM from the active emulation core and renders a 2x scaled preview."""
        vram_bytes = self.worker.get_memory_region(VRAM_ID)
        if not vram_bytes:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Could not read VRAM. Ensure game is actively running.")
            return

        img_data = np.zeros((768, 256), dtype=np.uint8) 
        max_tiles = min(len(vram_bytes) // 32, 3072) 
        
        if max_tiles > 0:
            arr = np.frombuffer(vram_bytes[:max_tiles*32], dtype=np.uint8).reshape((max_tiles, 8, 4))
            p1 = (arr & 0x0F) * 17        
            p2 = ((arr >> 4) & 0x0F) * 17 
            pixels = np.empty((max_tiles, 8, 8), dtype=np.uint8)
            pixels[:, :, 0::2] = p1
            pixels[:, :, 1::2] = p2
            for t in range(max_tiles):
                tile_x = (t % 32) * 8
                tile_y = (t // 32) * 8
                img_data[tile_y:tile_y+8, tile_x:tile_x+8] = pixels[t]

        # Convert numpy array to PyQt6 QImage
        height, width = img_data.shape
        bytes_per_line = width
        qimg = QImage(img_data.data, width, height, bytes_per_line, QImage.Format.Format_Grayscale8)
        
        # Scale 2x for better visibility in the tool
        pixmap = QPixmap.fromImage(qimg).scaled(width * 2, height * 2, 
                                                Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio, 
                                                Qt.TransformationMode.FastTransformation)
        self.image_label.setPixmap(pixmap)

    def export_vram_data(self):
        """Extracts VRAM, parses Palettes, and writes Colored/Raw PNGs + JSON Map to disk."""
        screenshots_dir = self.config.get("paths", "screenshots") or "screenshots"
        os.makedirs(screenshots_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        default_name = f"vram_export_{datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
        initial_path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), screenshots_dir, default_name)
        
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Save Complete VRAM Data Export", initial_path, "PNG Image (*.png)"
        )
        
        if not filepath:
            return # User canceled
            
        vram_bytes = self.worker.get_memory_region(VRAM_ID)
        if not vram_bytes:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", "VRAM Extraction Failed.")
            return
        
        img_data = np.zeros((768, 256), dtype=np.uint8) 
        max_tiles = min(len(vram_bytes) // 32, 3072) 
        
        # Decode raw tiles
        if max_tiles > 0:
            arr = np.frombuffer(vram_bytes[:max_tiles*32], dtype=np.uint8).reshape((max_tiles, 8, 4))
            p1 = (arr & 0x0F) * 17        
            p2 = ((arr >> 4) & 0x0F) * 17 
            pixels = np.empty((max_tiles, 8, 8), dtype=np.uint8)
            pixels[:, :, 0::2] = p1
            pixels[:, :, 1::2] = p2
            for t in range(max_tiles):
                tile_x = (t % 32) * 8
                tile_y = (t // 32) * 8
                img_data[tile_y:tile_y+8, tile_x:tile_x+8] = pixels[t]

        img_raw = Image.fromarray(img_data, 'L')
        
        # Extract Palette RAM (PRAM)
        pram_bytes = self.worker.get_memory_region(PRAM_ID)
        palette = np.zeros((256, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        
        if pram_bytes and len(pram_bytes) >= 32:
            pram_arr = np.frombuffer(pram_bytes[:32], dtype=np.uint16)
            for i in range(16):
                c = pram_arr[i]
                r = (c & 0x1F) << 3
                g = ((c >> 5) & 0x1F) << 3
                b = ((c >> 10) & 0x1F) << 3
                palette[i] = [r, g, b]
        else:
            colors = [[0,0,0], [0,128,0], [128,0,0], [0,0,128], [128,128,0]]
            for i in range(16): palette[i] = colors[i % len(colors)]

        # Apply Palette
        index_data = (img_data // 17).astype(np.uint8)
        img_colored = Image.fromarray(index_data, 'P')
        img_colored.putpalette(palette.flatten().tolist())

        # Build JSON Map
        tile_map = {}
        for t in range(max_tiles):
            tile_map[f"tile_{t}"] = {
                "index": t,
                "x_pixel": (t % 32) * 8,
                "y_pixel": (t // 32) * 8,
                "vram_address": f"0x{0x06000000 + t * 32:08X}"
            }
            
        base_path = filepath.rsplit('.', 1)[0] if '.' in filepath else filepath
        raw_path = base_path + "_raw.png"
        col_path = base_path + "_colored.png"
        json_path = base_path + "_map.json"
        
        img_raw.save(raw_path)
        img_colored.save(col_path)
        
        with open(json_path, "w") as f:
            json.dump(tile_map, f, indent=4)
            
        base_name = os.path.basename(filepath).replace(".png", "")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Exported", 
            f"Success! 3 Files created:\n\n1. {base_name}_raw.png\n2. {base_name}_colored.png\n3. {base_name}_map.json")

# GBA OAM Memory ID in mGBA libretro is typically 5 (or accessed via system ram offsets)
OAM_ID = 5 

class OAMViewerWindow(QMainWindow):
    """Replicates mGBA's Native Sprite (OAM) Viewer"""
    def __init__(self, worker, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.setWindowTitle("OAM (Sprite) Viewer")
        self.resize(600, 400)
        
        central = QWidget()
        self.setCentralWidget(central)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(central)
        
        self.refresh_btn = QPushButton("Refresh Sprite Data")
        self.refresh_btn.clicked.connect(self.refresh_oam)
        layout.addWidget(self.refresh_btn)
        
        self.info_label = QLabel("Click Refresh to dump active sprites.")
        self.info_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)
        self.info_label.setStyleSheet("font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;")
        
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setWidget(self.info_label)
        layout.addWidget(scroll)

    def refresh_oam(self):
        oam_bytes = self.worker.get_memory_region(OAM_ID)
        if not oam_bytes or len(oam_bytes) < 1024:
            self.info_label.setText("OAM Memory not exposed by this specific libretro core version.\n(Requires core compiled with advanced memory mapping).")
            return
            
        output = "ID  | X    | Y    | Tile | Palette\n"
        output += "-" * 40 + "\n"
        
        # Parse exactly like mGBA's GBAObj struct
        arr = np.frombuffer(oam_bytes[:1024], dtype=np.uint16)
        
        for i in range(128):
            attr0 = arr[i*4 + 0]
            attr1 = arr[i*4 + 1]
            attr2 = arr[i*4 + 2]
            
            y = attr0 & 0xFF
            x = attr1 & 0x1FF
            tile = attr2 & 0x3FF
            palette = (attr2 >> 12) & 0xF
            
            # Filter out hidden/disabled sprites (Y=160 or 254 usually means hidden in GBA)
            if y < 160:
                output += f"{i:<3} | {x:<4} | {y:<4} | {tile:<4} | {palette:<4}\n"
                
        self.info_label.setText(output)

{
    "audio": {
        "mute": true,
        "volume": 1.0,
        "low_pass_filter": "medium",
        "stereo_widener": true,
        "bass_boost": false,
        "soft_limiter": true
    },
    "emulation": {

# scene_dump.py
import os
import csv
import json
import struct
import hashlib
import datetime
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, 
                             QLabel, QPushButton, QFileDialog, 
                             QMessageBox, QProgressBar)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

try:
    from capstone import Cs, CS_ARCH_ARM, CS_MODE_ARM, CS_MODE_THUMB
    CAPSTONE_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    CAPSTONE_AVAILABLE = False


def bgr555_to_rgb(val: int):
    val = int(val)
    r = (val & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
    g = ((val >> 5) & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
    b = ((val >> 10) & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
    return (int(r), int(g), int(b))


class SceneDumpEngine:
    """Extracts, analyzes, and bundles complete GBA screen forensics and state snapshots."""
    def __init__(self, worker, config):
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config

    def create_compact_dump(self, rom_path: str) -> str:
        """Creates a forensic snapshot containing images, scene_data.json, and the exact .state file."""
        if not rom_path:
            return None

        # 1. Target directory: exports/<Game_Name>/dump_<Timestamp>/
        game_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(rom_path))[0]
        timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        
        base_export = self.config.get("paths", "exports") or "exports"
        dump_folder = os.path.join(base_export, game_name, f"dump_{timestamp}")
        layers_folder = os.path.join(dump_folder, "layers")
        
        os.makedirs(dump_folder, exist_ok=True)
        os.makedirs(layers_folder, exist_ok=True)

        # 2. Capture and Export the Exact Save State for this Frame
        state_filename = f"{game_name}_{timestamp}.state"
        state_path = os.path.join(dump_folder, state_filename)
        state_saved = False
        if self.worker and hasattr(self.worker, 'save_state'):
            state_saved = self.worker.save_state(state_path)

        # 3. Extract Memory Buffers
        vram = self.worker.get_memory_region("VRAM") or bytes(96 * 1024)
        pram = self.worker.get_memory_region("PRAM") or bytes(1024)
        oam = self.worker.get_memory_region("OAM") or bytes(1024)

        # 4. Extract Palettes
        bg_colors = []
        obj_colors = []
        if len(pram) >= 1024 and not all(b == 0 for b in pram[:1024]):
            words = struct.unpack("<512H", pram[:1024])
            bg_colors = [bgr555_to_rgb(w) for w in words[:256]]
            obj_colors = [bgr555_to_rgb(w) for w in words[256:]]
        else:
            frame = getattr(self.worker, 'latest_video_frame', None)
            if frame is not None:
                flat = frame.reshape(-1, 3)
                unique = np.unique(flat, axis=0)
                sampled = [(int(c[0]), int(c[1]), int(c[2])) for c in unique[:512]]
                while len(sampled) < 512: sampled.append((0, 0, 0))
                bg_colors = sampled[:256]
                obj_colors = sampled[256:]
            else:
                bg_colors = [(i, i, i) for i in range(256)]
                obj_colors = [(i, i, i) for i in range(256)]

        # 5. Save Composited Main Screen Capture
        raw_frame = getattr(self.worker, 'latest_video_frame', None)
        if raw_frame is not None:
            Image.fromarray(raw_frame, 'RGB').save(os.path.join(dump_folder, "screen.png"))

        # 6. Extract Only Meaningful Non-Empty Layer Data
        active_sbbs = self._detect_sbbs(vram)
        all_layer_tiles = {}
        layer_summaries = {}

        for bg_idx, bg_name in enumerate(["BG0", "BG1", "BG2", "BG3"]):
            sbb = active_sbbs[bg_idx] if bg_idx < len(active_sbbs) else (28 + bg_idx)
            cbb = bg_idx % 4

            layer_img, tile_data_list = self._render_and_map_bg(vram, bg_colors, sbb, cbb)
            non_empty_tiles = [t for t in tile_data_list if t["tile_dec"] != 0]

            if non_empty_tiles:
                layer_img.save(os.path.join(layers_folder, f"{bg_name.lower()}.png"))
                all_layer_tiles[bg_name] = non_empty_tiles
                layer_summaries[bg_name] = {
                    "sbb": sbb, 
                    "cbb": cbb,
                    "non_empty_tiles": len(non_empty_tiles)
                }

        # 7. Extract Visible Hardware Sprites (OAM)
        obj_img, sprite_list = self._render_and_map_sprites(vram, oam, obj_colors)
        visible_sprites = [s for s in sprite_list if s["active"]]
        if visible_sprites:
            obj_img.save(os.path.join(layers_folder, "obj.png"))

        # 8. Disassemble Rendering & DMA Routines from ROM
        asm_findings = self._disassemble_game_code()

        # 9. Compute ROM SHA1 Hash
        rom_sha1 = "unknown"
        if os.path.exists(rom_path):
            try:
                with open(rom_path, "rb") as rf:
                    rom_sha1 = hashlib.sha1(rf.read()).hexdigest()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 10. Export Self-Contained scene_data.json and Factual scene_description.md
        self._export_forensic_files(
            dump_folder, game_name, timestamp, rom_path, rom_sha1,
            state_filename, state_saved, layer_summaries, 
            all_layer_tiles, visible_sprites, asm_findings
        )

        return dump_folder

    def _export_forensic_files(self, dump_folder, game_name, timestamp, rom_path, rom_sha1,
                               state_filename, state_saved, layer_summaries, 
                               all_layer_tiles, visible_sprites, asm_findings):
        """Writes comprehensive scene_data.json and factual scene_description.md."""
        
        # Group non-empty tiles into horizontal text lines
        text_lines = []
        for layer_name, tiles in all_layer_tiles.items():
            rows = {}
            for t in tiles:
                rows.setdefault(t["row"], []).append(t)

            for r, row_tiles in rows.items():
                if len(row_tiles) >= 2:
                    sorted_row = sorted(row_tiles, key=lambda it: it["col"])
                    hex_seq = " ".join(t["tile_hex"] for t in sorted_row)
                    text_lines.append({
                        "layer": layer_name,
                        "row": r,
                        "y_px": r * 8,
                        "start_x_px": sorted_row[0]["x"],
                        "count": len(sorted_row),
                        "hex_sequence": hex_seq,
                        "vram_start": sorted_row[0]["vram_addr"]
                    })

        # =====================================================================
        # 1. Fully Self-Contained scene_data.json
        # =====================================================================
        comprehensive_data = {
            "game": game_name,
            "rom_file": os.path.basename(rom_path),
            "rom_sha1": rom_sha1,
            "timestamp": timestamp,
            "resolution": "240x160",
            "save_state": {
                "file": state_filename,
                "saved_successfully": state_saved,
                "path_relative": state_filename
            },
            "active_layers": layer_summaries,
            "detected_text_lines": text_lines,
            "visible_sprites": [
                {
                    "id": s["id"], 
                    "pos": [s["x"], s["y"]],
                    "size": f"{s['width']}x{s['height']}",
                    "tile": s["tile_hex"], 
                    "pal": s["pal_bank"],
                    "priority": s.get("priority", 0)
                }
                for s in visible_sprites
            ],
            "disassembly": asm_findings
        }

        with open(os.path.join(dump_folder, "scene_data.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(comprehensive_data, f, indent=2)

        # =====================================================================
        # 2. Factual scene_description.md (Strict Technical Report, No Prompting)
        # =====================================================================
        md_text = f"""# GBA Screen Forensic Analysis

## 1. Frame Metadata
- **Game Title**: `{game_name}`
- **ROM File**: `{os.path.basename(rom_path)}`
- **ROM SHA1**: `{rom_sha1}`
- **Captured Timestamp**: `{timestamp}`
- **Resolution**: 240×160 pixels
- **Save State Snapshot**: `{state_filename}` (Loads this exact emulation state)
- **Visual Assets**:
  - `screen.png`: Full composited game screen
  - `layers/`: Isolated active background layers and hardware sprites

---

## 2. Detected On-Screen Text & Tile Sequences
Horizontal tile sequences detected across active backgrounds:

| Layer | Row (Y) | Start X | Tile Count | Tile ID Sequence (Hex) | VRAM Start Addr |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
"""
        for line in text_lines[:25]:
            md_text += f"| {line['layer']} | Row {line['row']} ({line['y_px']}px) | {line['start_x_px']}px | {line['count']} | `{line['hex_sequence']}` | `{line['vram_start']}` |\n"

        md_text += f"""
---

## 3. Visible Hardware Sprites (OAM)
Total visible active sprites: **{len(visible_sprites)}**

| Sprite ID | Position (X, Y) | Dimensions | Base Tile | Palette Bank | Priority |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
"""
        for s in visible_sprites[:20]:
            md_text += f"| #{s['id']:03d} | ({s['x']}, {s['y']}) | {s['width']}×{s['height']} | `{s['tile_hex']}` | Bank {s['pal_bank']} | {s.get('priority', 0)} |\n"

        md_text += """
---

## 4. Hardware Disassembly (VRAM / DMA Subroutines)
```arm
"""
        if asm_findings.get("vram_routines"):
            for i, blk in enumerate(asm_findings["vram_routines"][:2]):
                md_text += f"; --- VRAM Transfer Subroutine #{i+1} ---\n"
                md_text += "\n".join(blk) + "\n\n"
        else:
            md_text += "; No direct VRAM routines scanned.\n"

        if asm_findings.get("dma_routines"):
            md_text += "; --- DMA Controller Subroutines ---\n"
            for blk in asm_findings["dma_routines"][:1]:
                md_text += "\n".join(blk) + "\n\n"

        md_text += "```\n"

        with open(os.path.join(dump_folder, "scene_description.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(md_text)

    def _detect_sbbs(self, vram):
        active = []
        for sbb in range(32):
            chunk = vram[sbb * 0x800:(sbb + 1) * 0x800]
            if any(chunk):
                words = struct.unpack("<1024H", chunk)
                if len(set(w & 0x3FF for w in words)) > 3:
                    active.append(sbb)
        return active

    def _render_and_map_bg(self, vram, bg_colors, sbb, cbb):
        img = Image.new("RGBA", (240, 160), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        tile_data_list = []
        sbb_offset = sbb * 0x800
        cbb_offset = cbb * 0x4000
        if sbb_offset + 0x800 > len(vram): return img, tile_data_list

        entries = struct.unpack("<1024H", vram[sbb_offset:sbb_offset + 0x800])

        for ty in range(20):
            for tx in range(30):
                entry = entries[ty * 32 + tx]
                tile_idx = entry & 0x3FF
                h_flip = bool(entry & (1 << 10))
                v_flip = bool(entry & (1 << 11))
                pal_bank = (entry >> 12) & 0xF

                tile_vram_addr = 0x06000000 + cbb_offset + (tile_idx * 32)
                tile_data_list.append({
                    "row": ty, "col": tx, "x": tx * 8, "y": ty * 8,
                    "tile_dec": tile_idx, "tile_hex": f"0x{tile_idx:03X}",
                    "vram_addr": f"0x{tile_vram_addr:08X}",
                    "pal_bank": pal_bank, "h_flip": h_flip, "v_flip": v_flip
                })

                t_addr = cbb_offset + (tile_idx * 32)
                if t_addr + 32 > len(vram): continue
                tile_bytes = vram[t_addr:t_addr + 32]

                for py in range(8):
                    for px in range(8):
                        actual_px = (7 - px) if h_flip else px
                        actual_py = (7 - py) if v_flip else py

                        b = tile_bytes[actual_py * 4 + actual_px // 2]
                        c_idx = (b & 0xF) if (actual_px % 2 == 0) else ((b >> 4) & 0xF)
                        if c_idx != 0:
                            rgb = bg_colors[(pal_bank * 16 + c_idx) % len(bg_colors)]
                            img.putpixel((tx * 8 + px, ty * 8 + py), (*rgb, 255))

        return img, tile_data_list

    def _render_and_map_sprites(self, vram, oam, obj_colors):
        img = Image.new("RGBA", (240, 160), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        sprite_list = []
        if len(oam) < 1024: return img, sprite_list

        words = struct.unpack("<512H", oam[:1024])
        SPRITE_SIZES = {
            0: [(8, 8), (16, 16), (32, 32), (64, 64)],
            1: [(16, 8), (32, 8), (32, 16), (64, 32)],
            2: [(8, 16), (8, 32), (16, 32), (32, 64)]
        }

        for i in range(128):
            a0, a1, a2 = words[i*4], words[i*4+1], words[i*4+2]
            y = a0 & 0xFF
            shape = (a0 >> 14) & 0x3
            x = a1 & 0x1FF
            if x >= 240: x -= 512
            size_idx = (a1 >> 14) & 0x3
            w, h = SPRITE_SIZES.get(shape, [(8, 8)]*4)[size_idx]
            tile = a2 & 0x3FF
            pal = (a2 >> 12) & 0xF
            h_flip = bool(a1 & (1 << 12))
            v_flip = bool(a1 & (1 << 13))
            pri = (a2 >> 10) & 0x3
            is_active = (y < 160 and x < 240 and x + w > 0)

            sprite_list.append({
                "id": i, "active": is_active, "x": x, "y": y,
                "width": w, "height": h, "tile_dec": tile,
                "tile_hex": f"0x{tile:03X}", "pal_bank": pal,
                "priority": pri, "h_flip": h_flip, "v_flip": v_flip
            })

            if not is_active: continue

            tx_count, ty_count = w // 8, h // 8
            for ty in range(ty_count):
                for tx in range(tx_count):
                    t_idx = tile + (ty * 32) + tx
                    t_addr = 0x10000 + (t_idx * 32)
                    if t_addr + 32 > len(vram): continue
                    tile_bytes = vram[t_addr:t_addr + 32]

                    for py in range(8):
                        for px in range(8):
                            actual_px = (w - 1 - (tx * 8 + px)) if h_flip else (tx * 8 + px)
                            actual_py = (h - 1 - (ty * 8 + py)) if v_flip else (ty * 8 + py)

                            if not (0 <= x + actual_px < 240 and 0 <= y + actual_py < 160):
                                continue

                            b = tile_bytes[py * 4 + px // 2]
                            c_idx = (b & 0xF) if (px % 2 == 0) else ((b >> 4) & 0xF)
                            if c_idx != 0:
                                rgb = obj_colors[(pal * 16 + c_idx) % len(obj_colors)]
                                img.putpixel((x + actual_px, y + actual_py), (*rgb, 255))

        return img, sprite_list

    def _disassemble_game_code(self) -> dict:
        findings = {
            "entry_point": [],
            "vram_routines": [],
            "dma_routines": [],
            "capstone_installed": CAPSTONE_AVAILABLE
        }
        if not self.worker or not self.worker.rom_path or not os.path.exists(self.worker.rom_path) or not CAPSTONE_AVAILABLE:
            return findings

        with open(self.worker.rom_path, "rb") as f:
            rom_data = f.read(min(1024 * 1024, os.path.getsize(self.worker.rom_path)))

        cs_thumb = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM, CS_MODE_THUMB)
        cs_arm = Cs(CS_ARCH_ARM, CS_MODE_ARM)

        try:
            for insn in cs_arm.disasm(rom_data[0:64], 0x08000000):
                findings["entry_point"].append(f"{insn.address:#010x}:  {insn.mnemonic:<8} {insn.op_str}")
        except Exception: pass

        vram_pattern = struct.pack("<I", 0x06000000)
        pos = 0
        while len(findings["vram_routines"]) < 3:
            found = rom_data.find(vram_pattern, pos)
            if found == -1: break
            fn_start = max(0, found - 20)
            addr = 0x08000000 + fn_start
            routine_block = []
            for insn in cs_thumb.disasm(rom_data[fn_start:fn_start + 40], addr):
                routine_block.append(f"{insn.address:#010x}:  {insn.mnemonic:<8} {insn.op_str}")
            if routine_block: findings["vram_routines"].append(routine_block)
            pos = found + 4

        dma_pattern = struct.pack("<I", 0x040000B0)
        found_dma = rom_data.find(dma_pattern)
        if found_dma != -1:
            fn_start = max(0, found_dma - 16)
            addr = 0x08000000 + fn_start
            routine_block = []
            for insn in cs_thumb.disasm(rom_data[fn_start:fn_start + 48], addr):
                routine_block.append(f"{insn.address:#010x}:  {insn.mnemonic:<8} {insn.op_str}")
            findings["dma_routines"].append(routine_block)

        return findings


class SceneDumpDialog(QDialog):
    """GUI Trigger dialog with progress bar."""
    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.engine = SceneDumpEngine(worker, config)

        self.setWindowTitle("Export Full Screen Forensic Dossier")
        self.resize(560, 310)
        self._init_ui()

    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        desc = QLabel(
            "<b style='color: #00FF66; font-size: 14px;'>Forensic Screen &amp; State Snapshot Studio</b><br><br>"
            "Captures a complete, self-contained forensic dossier of this frame:<br>"
            "• Exact emulator save state snapshot (<code>.state</code> file)<br>"
            "• Complete technical summary in <code>scene_data.json</code><br>"
            "• Factual hardware report in <code>scene_description.md</code><br>"
            "• Visual Layer PNGs and composite <code>screen.png</code>"
        )
        desc.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                background-color: #1e1e1e;
                color: #e0e0e0;
                padding: 12px;
                border-radius: 8px;
                border: 1px solid #333333;
                font-size: 13px;
                line-height: 1.4;
            }
            code {
                background-color: #2b2b2b;
                color: #00FF66;
                padding: 2px 4px;
                border-radius: 4px;
                font-family: monospace;
            }
        """)
        layout.addWidget(desc)

        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.progress_bar.setRange(0, 100)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
        layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)

        self.status_lbl = QLabel("Ready to export.")
        self.status_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #888; font-weight: bold;")
        layout.addWidget(self.status_lbl)

        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        export_btn = QPushButton("🚀 Generate Forensic Dossier...")
        export_btn.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #4CAF50;
                color: white;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 14px;
                padding: 10px;
                border-radius: 6px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #45a049;
            }
        """)
        export_btn.clicked.connect(self.run_export)
        btn_row.addWidget(export_btn)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

    def run_export(self):
        rom_path = self.worker.rom_path if self.worker else None
        if not rom_path:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "No game ROM currently running.")
            return

        try:
            self.progress_bar.setValue(40)
            self.status_lbl.setText("Dumping memory, state, and rendering layers...")
            self.repaint()

            out_dir = self.engine.create_compact_dump(rom_path)
            self.progress_bar.setValue(100)
            self.status_lbl.setText("Done!")

            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "Export Complete", 
                f"Forensic dossier and save state exported to:\n{out_dir}\n\n"
                f"Exported Files:\n"
                f"• *.state (Exact save state for this frame)\n"
                f"• scene_data.json (Complete machine-readable forensics)\n"
                f"• scene_description.md (Factual report)\n"
                f"• screen.png & layers/ (Visual assets)"
            )
            self.accept()
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Export Failed", f"An error occurred: {e}")

--- END OF FILE scene_dump.py ---

--- START OF FILE session_recorder.py ---

# session_recorder.py
import os
import json
from datetime import datetime

# Libretro GBA Button Mapping
ID_TO_BTN = {
    8: "A",
    0: "B",
    2: "SELECT",
    3: "START",
    4: "UP",
    5: "DOWN",
    6: "LEFT",
    7: "RIGHT",
    10: "L",
    11: "R"
}

BTN_TO_ID = {v: k for k, v in ID_TO_BTN.items()}


class SessionRecorder:
    """Records gameplay inputs into AI-friendly JSON and replays them deterministically."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.mode = "IDLE"  # "IDLE", "RECORDING", "PLAYBACK"
        self.rom_path = None
        self.start_timestamp = None

        # Recording state
        self.recorded_events = []
        self.last_buttons = None
        self.last_paused = None
        self.initial_state_filename = None

        # Playback state
        self.playback_timeline = {}
        self.playback_max_frame = 0
        self.current_playback_frame = 0
        self.active_playback_buttons = set()

    @property
    def is_recording(self) -> bool:
        return self.mode == "RECORDING"

    @property
    def is_playing(self) -> bool:
        return self.mode == "PLAYBACK"

    # =========================================================================
    # RECORDING PIPELINE
    # =========================================================================
    def start_recording(self, worker, rom_path: str, base_export_dir: str = "replays") -> str:
        """Captures an initial snapshot state and starts frame logging."""
        os.makedirs(base_export_dir, exist_ok=True)
        self.rom_path = rom_path
        self.recorded_events = []
        self.last_buttons = None
        self.last_paused = None
        self.start_timestamp = datetime.now()

        tag = self.start_timestamp.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        rom_base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(rom_path))[0]

        # 1. Save base snapshot state for deterministic replay
        self.initial_state_filename = f"{rom_base}_{tag}.state"
        full_state_path = os.path.join(base_export_dir, self.initial_state_filename)

        saved = worker.save_state(full_state_path)
        if not saved:
            self.mode = "IDLE"
            return None

        self.mode = "RECORDING"
        return full_state_path

    def record_frame(self, frame_num: int, active_buttons: list, is_paused: bool):
        """Called every frame tick from core_worker. Stores input deltas only."""
        if not self.is_recording:
            return

        sorted_btns = sorted(list(set(active_buttons)))

        if (sorted_btns != self.last_buttons) or (is_paused != self.last_paused):
            self.recorded_events.append({
                "f": frame_num,
                "btn": sorted_btns,
                "paused": is_paused
            })
            self.last_buttons = sorted_btns
            self.last_paused = is_paused

    def stop_recording(self, total_frames: int, base_export_dir: str = "replays") -> str:
        """Generates the AI-friendly structured JSON file."""
        if not self.is_recording:
            return None

        self.mode = "IDLE"
        tag = self.start_timestamp.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        rom_base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(self.rom_path))[0]
        json_filename = f"{rom_base}_{tag}.replay.json"
        full_json_path = os.path.join(base_export_dir, json_filename)

        summary_actions = self._build_summary_actions(total_frames)

        payload = {
            "format": "gba_replay_v1",
            "metadata": {
                "game_title": rom_base,
                "rom_file": os.path.basename(self.rom_path),
                "recorded_at": self.start_timestamp.isoformat(),
                "fps": 59.7275,
                "total_frames": total_frames,
                "duration_seconds": round(total_frames / 59.7275, 2),
                "initial_state_file": self.initial_state_filename
            },
            "ai_documentation": {
                "description": "Deterministic GBA gameplay log. 'summary_actions' groups sustained user actions into semantic intervals for LLMs. 'sparse_timeline' contains frame-accurate input changes.",
                "button_legend": ["A", "B", "SELECT", "START", "UP", "DOWN", "LEFT", "RIGHT", "L", "R"]
            },
            "summary_actions": summary_actions,
            "sparse_timeline": self.recorded_events
        }

        with open(full_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(payload, f, indent=2)

        return full_json_path

    def _build_summary_actions(self, total_frames: int) -> list:
        """Converts frame deltas into continuous intervals with semantic descriptions."""
        actions = []
        if not self.recorded_events:
            return actions

        for i, ev in enumerate(self.recorded_events):
            start = ev["f"]
            end = self.recorded_events[i + 1]["f"] - 1 if (i + 1 < len(self.recorded_events)) else total_frames
            btns = ev["btn"]
            is_paused = ev.get("paused", False)

            if is_paused:
                desc = "Emulator Paused"
            elif not btns:
                desc = "Idle / Neutral"
            else:
                desc = f"Hold [{', '.join(btns)}]"

            actions.append({
                "start_frame": start,
                "end_frame": end,
                "duration_frames": max(1, (end - start) + 1),
                "buttons": btns,
                "action": desc
            })
        return actions

    # =========================================================================
    # PLAYBACK PIPELINE
    # =========================================================================
    def load_replay(self, json_path: str, worker) -> bool:
        """Loads JSON, restores the frame 0 state snapshot, and sets up playback."""
        if not os.path.exists(json_path):
            return False

        with open(json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)

        meta = data.get("metadata", {})
        state_file = meta.get("initial_state_file")
        if not state_file:
            return False

        json_dir = os.path.dirname(json_path)
        full_state_path = os.path.join(json_dir, state_file)
        if not os.path.exists(full_state_path):
            full_state_path = os.path.join("replays", state_file)
            if not os.path.exists(full_state_path):
                return False

        if not worker.load_state(full_state_path):
            return False

        self.playback_timeline = {}
        for ev in data.get("sparse_timeline", []):
            self.playback_timeline[ev["f"]] = {
                "btn": set(ev["btn"]),
                "paused": ev.get("paused", False)
            }

        self.playback_max_frame = meta.get("total_frames", 0)
        self.current_playback_frame = 0
        self.active_playback_buttons = set()
        self.mode = "PLAYBACK"
        return True

    def step_playback(self, frame_num: int) -> tuple:
        """Returns (active_buttons_set, should_pause, is_finished)."""
        if not self.is_playing:
            return set(), False, True

        if frame_num in self.playback_timeline:
            ev = self.playback_timeline[frame_num]
            self.active_playback_buttons = ev["btn"]
            if ev["paused"]:
                return self.active_playback_buttons, True, False

        self.current_playback_frame = frame_num

        if frame_num >= self.playback_max_frame:
            self.mode = "IDLE"
            return set(), False, True

        return self.active_playback_buttons, False, False

    def stop_playback(self):
        self.mode = "IDLE"
        self.active_playback_buttons = set()

--- END OF FILE session_recorder.py ---

:run_silent

--- START OF FILE start.ps1 ---

# start.ps1 - PowerShell Launcher for mGBA Python Emulator
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
Set-Location $PSScriptRoot

$pythonExe = $null

# 1. Probe virtual environments
$venvCandidates = @(
    ".venv\Scripts\python.exe",
    "venv\Scripts\python.exe",
    "ENV\Scripts\python.exe",
    "env\Scripts\python.exe"
)

foreach ($candidate in $venvCandidates) {
    $fullPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot $candidate
    if (Test-Path $fullPath) {
        $pythonExe = $fullPath
        break
    }
}

# 2. Probe system PATH
if (-not $pythonExe) {
    $pyCmd = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($pyCmd) {
        $pythonExe = $pyCmd.Source
    } else {
        $pyLauncher = Get-Command py -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        if ($pyLauncher) {
            $pythonExe = $pyLauncher.Source
        }
    }
}

if (-not $pythonExe) {
    Write-Host "[ERROR] Python was not found in your PATH or in any local virtual environment (.venv/venv)." -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host "Please install Python 3.10+ and run: pip install -r requirements.txt" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Read-Host "Press Enter to exit"
    exit 1
}

Write-Host "Starting mGBA Python Emulator..." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Using interpreter: $pythonExe" -ForegroundColor DarkGray

try {
    & $pythonExe main.py $args
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Write-Host "`n[NOTICE] The application exited with code $LASTEXITCODE." -ForegroundColor Yellow
        Read-Host "Press Enter to exit"
    }
} catch {
    Write-Host "`n[CRASH DETECTED] An error occurred while executing the emulator:" -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Read-Host "Press Enter to exit"
}

--- END OF FILE start.ps1 ---

--- START OF FILE start.sh ---

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")"

# 1. Search for Python in local virtual environments or system PATH
PYTHON_BIN=""

if [ -f ".venv/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON_BIN=".venv/bin/python"
elif [ -f "venv/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON_BIN="venv/bin/python"
elif [ -f "ENV/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON_BIN="ENV/bin/python"
elif [ -f "env/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON_BIN="env/bin/python"
elif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PYTHON_BIN="python3"
elif command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PYTHON_BIN="python"
fi

if [ -z "$PYTHON_BIN" ]; then
    echo "[ERROR] Python 3 was not found in PATH or a local virtual environment (.venv/venv)."
    echo "Please install Python 3 and ensure the required packages from requirements.txt are installed."
    exit 1
fi

# 2. Check for background launch flag
if [ "$1" = "--background" ] || [ "$1" = "-b" ]; then
    echo "Starting mGBA Emulator in background..."
    nohup "$PYTHON_BIN" main.py "$@" > startup.log 2>&1 &
    echo "Logs are being redirected to startup.log"
else
    echo "Starting mGBA Python Emulator using: $PYTHON_BIN"
    exec "$PYTHON_BIN" main.py "$@"
fi
