```python
    # =========================================================================
    # SENSORS & MOTION SLOTS
    # =========================================================================
    def toggle_pause_on_unfocus(self):
        val = self.pause_unfocus_action.isChecked()
        self.config.set("emulation", "pause_on_unfocus", val)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Pause When Unfocused: {'ON' if val else 'OFF'}")

    def toggle_sensor_hud(self):
        self.video_widget.show_sensor_hud = self.act_sensor_hud.isChecked()
        self.video_widget.update()

    def set_solar_level(self, level: int):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.set_solar_level(level)
            self.video_widget.current_solar_level = level
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"☀️ Solar Sensor: Level {level}/10")
            self.video_widget.update()

    def adjust_solar_sensor(self, delta: int):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.step_solar_level(delta)
            cur = self.worker.solar_level
            self.video_widget.current_solar_level = cur
            if cur in self.solar_actions:
                self.solar_actions[cur].setChecked(True)
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"☀️ Solar Sensor: Level {cur}/10")
            self.video_widget.update()

    def toggle_rumble(self):
        val = self.act_rumble.isChecked()
        self.config.set("sensors", "rumble_enabled", val)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Rumble Motor: {'ON' if val else 'OFF'}")

    def toggle_mouse_gyro(self):
        val = self.act_mouse_gyro.isChecked()
        self.config.set("sensors", "mouse_gyro", val)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Mouse Gyro Drag: {'ON' if val else 'OFF'}")

    def reset_sensors(self):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.reset_sensors()
            self.video_widget.current_tilt_x = 0.0
            self.video_widget.current_tilt_y = 0.0
            self.video_widget.current_gyro_angle = 0.0
            self.video_widget.show_osd("🎯 Gyro/Tilt Centered (0°)")
            self.video_widget.update()

    def on_tilt_updated(self, x: float, y: float, angle: float):
        self.video_widget.current_tilt_x = x
        self.video_widget.current_tilt_y = y
        self.video_widget.current_gyro_angle = angle
        self.video_widget.update()

    def on_solar_updated(self, level: int):
        self.video_widget.current_solar_level = level
        if level in self.solar_actions:
            self.solar_actions[level].setChecked(True)
        self.video_widget.update()

    # =========================================================================
    # REPLAY & EMULATION SLOTS
    # =========================================================================
    def toggle_session_recording(self):
        if not self.worker or not self.current_rom:
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Error: No ROM running.")
            return

        replays_dir = self.config.get("paths", "replays") or "replays"

        if not self.worker.recorder.is_recording:
            saved_state_path = self.worker.start_recording_session(replays_dir)
            if saved_state_path:
                self.act_record_session.setText("⏹ Stop Recording Session")
                self.video_widget.show_osd("⏺ RECORDING STARTED", 3000)
            else:
                self.video_widget.show_osd("Error: Could not save initial state.")
        else:
            json_path = self.worker.stop_recording_session(replays_dir)
            self.act_record_session.setText("⏺ Start Recording Session...")
            if json_path:
                self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Saved Replay: {os.path.basename(json_path)}", 4000)
                QMessageBox.information(
                    self, "Session Recorded", 
                    f"Gameplay session and initial snapshot saved successfully!\n\n"
                    f"Replay File (AI-Friendly JSON):\n{json_path}"
                )

    def load_replay_session(self):
        if not self.worker or not self.current_rom:
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Error: Load ROM first.")
            return

        replays_dir = self.config.get("paths", "replays") or "replays"
        os.makedirs(replays_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "Load Replay Session", replays_dir, "Replay Files (*.replay.json *.json)"
        )
        if not filepath:
            return

        success = self.worker.start_replay_session(filepath)
        if success:
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"▶ REPLAYING: {os.path.basename(filepath)}", 3000)
        else:
            QMessageBox.critical(
                self, "Replay Error", 
                "Failed to start replay. Ensure the corresponding .state file exists in the same folder."
            )

    def stop_replay_session(self):
        if self.worker and self.worker.recorder.is_playing:
            self.worker.stop_replay_session()
            self.video_widget.show_osd("⏹ Replay Stopped. Control Restored.")

    def on_replay_finished(self):
        self.video_widget.show_osd("🏁 Replay Complete! Control Restored.", 3000)

    def trigger_ai_translation(self):
        if not self.worker or not self.current_rom or self.video_widget.current_frame_data is None:
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Error: Game not running.")
            return

        if self.trans_worker and self.trans_worker.isRunning():
            self.video_widget.show_osd("AI Translation in progress...")
            return

        self.video_widget.show_osd("🔍 Running AI Screen Translation...")

        self.trans_worker = TranslationWorker(
            frame_data=self.video_widget.current_frame_data.copy(),
            rom_path=self.current_rom,
            config=self.config
        )
        self.trans_worker.finished.connect(self.on_ai_translation_finished)
        self.trans_worker.error.connect(lambda err: QMessageBox.warning(self, "AI Translation Error", err))
        self.trans_worker.start()

    def on_ai_translation_finished(self, boxes: list, msg: str):
        if boxes:
            blocks_html = []
            for b in boxes:
                orig = html.escape((b.original or "").strip())
                trans = html.escape((b.translated or "").strip())
                if not orig or not trans:
                    continue

                cat = getattr(b, "category", "dialogue")
                sp_orig = html.escape(getattr(b, "speaker_orig", "") or "")
                sp_trans = html.escape(getattr(b, "speaker_trans", "") or "")

                if cat == "dialogue":
                    accent = "#64B5F6" if sp_orig else "#BA68C8"
                    if sp_orig and sp_trans and sp_trans.lower() != sp_orig.lower():
                        speaker_html = (
                            f'<div style="color: #FFD54F; font-weight: bold; font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 2px;">'
                            f'💬 {sp_trans} <span style="color: #90A4AE; font-weight: normal;">({sp_orig})</span></div>'
                        )
                    elif sp_orig:
                        speaker_html = (
                            f'<div style="color: #FFD54F; font-weight: bold; font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 2px;">'
                            f'💬 {sp_orig}</div>'
                        )
                    else:
                        speaker_html = ""

                    blocks_html.append(
                        f'<table width="100%" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="margin-bottom: 6px;">'
                        f'<tr>'
                        f'<td width="4" bgcolor="{accent}"></td>'
                        f'<td bgcolor="#181C24" style="padding: 6px 10px;">'
                        f'{speaker_html}'
                        f'<div style="color: #90CAF9; font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 2px;">{orig}</div>'
                        f'<div style="color: #A5D6A7; font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px;">→ {trans}</div>'
                        f'</td>'
                        f'</tr></table>'
                    )

                elif cat == "menu":
                    blocks_html.append(
                        f'<div style="margin-bottom: 3px; padding: 2px 4px;">'
                        f'<span style="background-color: #1E3A44; color: #4DD0E1; font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold;">'
                        f'&nbsp;MENU&nbsp;</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;'
                        f'<span style="color: #B0BEC5;">{orig}</span> '
                        f'<span style="color: #78909C;">→</span> '
                        f'<span style="color: #81C784; font-weight: bold;">{trans}</span>'
                        f'</div>'
                    )

                else:
                    blocks_html.append(
                        f'<div style="margin-bottom: 4px; padding: 2px 4px;">'
                        f'<span style="background-color: #3E2C18; color: #FFB74D; font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold;">'
                        f'&nbsp;LABEL&nbsp;</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;'
                        f'<span style="color: #FFE082;">{orig}</span> '
                        f'<span style="color: #78909C;">→</span> '
                        f'<span style="color: #FFD54F; font-weight: bold;">{trans}</span>'
                        f'</div>'
                    )

            self.translation_panel.setHtml("".join(blocks_html))
            sb = self.translation_panel.verticalScrollBar()
            sb.setValue(0)

        self.video_widget.show_osd(msg, 3000)

    def open_ai_translation_config(self):
        dlg = AITranslationConfigDialog(self.config, self)
        dlg.exec()

    def trigger_instant_scene_dump(self):
        if not self.worker or not self.current_rom:
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Error: No active ROM to dump")
            return
        try:
            from scene_dump import SceneDumpEngine
            engine = SceneDumpEngine(self.worker, self.config)
            dump_folder = engine.create_compact_dump(self.current_rom)
            short_path = os.path.basename(dump_folder)
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"AI Dossier Dumped: {short_path}", 3000)
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Dump Error", f"Failed to create scene dump: {e}")

    def update_recent_menu(self):
        self.recent_menu.clear()
        recent_files = self.config.get_recent_files()

        if not recent_files:
            no_recents = QAction("No Recent Files", self)
            no_recents.setEnabled(False)
            self.recent_menu.addAction(no_recents)
            return

        for path in recent_files:
            file_name = os.path.basename(path)
            action = QAction(f"{file_name} ({path})", self)
            action.triggered.connect(lambda checked, p=path: self.load_rom_file(p))
            self.recent_menu.addAction(action)

        self.recent_menu.addSeparator()
        clear_act = QAction("Clear Recent Files", self)
        clear_act.triggered.connect(self.clear_recent_files)
        self.recent_menu.addAction(clear_act)

    def clear_recent_files(self):
        self.config.clear_recent_files()
        self.update_recent_menu()
        self.video_widget.show_osd("Cleared Recent Files")

    def _sync_ui_with_config(self):
        self.mute_action.setChecked(bool(self.config.get("audio", "mute")))
        
        lpf = str(self.config.get("audio", "low_pass_filter") or "medium").lower()
        if lpf in self.lpf_actions:
            self.lpf_actions[lpf].setChecked(True)
        self.widener_action.setChecked(bool(self.config.get("audio", "stereo_widener")))
        self.bass_action.setChecked(bool(self.config.get("audio", "bass_boost")))
        self.limiter_action.setChecked(bool(self.config.get("audio", "soft_limiter")))

        raw_vol = self.config.get("audio", "volume")
        current_vol = float(raw_vol) if raw_vol is not None else 0.5
        if current_vol > 2.0:
            current_vol = 0.5
            self.config.set("audio", "volume", 0.5)

        closest_vol = min(self.vol_actions.keys(), key=lambda v: abs(v - current_vol))
        self.vol_actions[closest_vol].setChecked(True)

        self.skip_bios_action.setChecked(bool(self.config.get("emulation", "skip_bios")))
        self.blend_action.setChecked(bool(self.config.get("video", "interframe_blending")))
        self.pause_unfocus_action.setChecked(bool(self.config.get("emulation", "pause_on_unfocus", True)))

        self.current_slot = int(self.config.get("emulation", "state_slot") or 1)
        if self.current_slot in self.slot_actions:
            self.slot_actions[self.current_slot].setChecked(True)

        current_ff = float(self.config.get("emulation", "fast_forward_ratio") or 2.0)
        for spd, act in self.ff_actions.items():
            if abs(spd - current_ff) < 0.01:
                act.setChecked(True)
                break

        current_fs = int(self.config.get("video", "frameskip") or 0)
        if current_fs in self.fs_actions:
            self.fs_actions[current_fs].setChecked(True)

        solar_lvl = int(self.config.get("sensors", "solar_level") if self.config.get("sensors") else 5)
        if solar_lvl in self.solar_actions:
            self.solar_actions[solar_lvl].setChecked(True)
        self.video_widget.current_solar_level = solar_lvl

        self._build_keymap()

    def set_lpf_mode(self, mode: str):
        self.config.set("audio", "low_pass_filter", mode)
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.update_audio_settings()
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Low-Pass Filter: {mode.capitalize()}")

    def toggle_stereo_widener(self):
        val = self.widener_action.isChecked()
        self.config.set("audio", "stereo_widener", val)
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.update_audio_settings()
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Stereo Widener: {'ON' if val else 'OFF'}")

    def toggle_bass_boost(self):
        val = self.bass_action.isChecked()
        self.config.set("audio", "bass_boost", val)
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.update_audio_settings()
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Bass Boost: {'ON' if val else 'OFF'}")

    def toggle_soft_limiter(self):
        val = self.limiter_action.isChecked()
        self.config.set("audio", "soft_limiter", val)
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.update_audio_settings()
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Soft Limiter: {'ON' if val else 'OFF'}")

    def set_volume_gain(self, vol: float):
        self.config.set("audio", "volume", vol)
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.update_audio_settings()
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Volume: {int(vol * 100)}%")

    def toggle_mute(self):
        self.config.set("audio", "mute", self.mute_action.isChecked())
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.update_audio_settings()
        self.video_widget.show_osd("Audio settings applied")

    def reset_settings_to_default(self):
        confirm = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "Reset Settings",
            "Are you sure you want to reset all settings, controls, sensors, and audio options back to default?",
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes | QMessageBox.StandardButton.No,
            QMessageBox.StandardButton.No
        )
        if confirm == QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
            self.config.reset_to_defaults()
            self._sync_ui_with_config()
            self.update_recent_menu()
            if self.worker:
                self.worker.update_audio_settings()
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Settings Reset to Default")
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", "All settings have been restored to factory defaults.")

    def _open_dev_tool(self, dialog_cls):
        if not self.worker:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Notice", "Emulation must be running to inspect game state.")
            return
        self.active_tool_window = dialog_cls(self.worker, self.config, self)
        self.active_tool_window.show()

    def open_graphic_editor(self):
        if not self.worker:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Notice", "Emulation must be running to edit graphics.")
            return
        from graphic_editor import GraphicEditorDialog
        self.active_tool_window = GraphicEditorDialog(self.worker, self.config, self)
        self.active_tool_window.show()

    def open_translation_tools(self):
        if not self.worker:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Notice", "Emulation must be running to inspect memory.")
            return
        try:
            from translation_tools import TranslationToolsDialog
            self.active_tool_window = TranslationToolsDialog(self.worker, self.config, self)
            self.active_tool_window.show()
        except ImportError as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to load translation tools: {e}")

    def open_font_tools(self):
        if not self.worker:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Notice", "Emulation must be running to scan fonts.")
            return
        try:
            from font_tools import FontToolsDialog
            self.active_tool_window = FontToolsDialog(self.worker, self.config, self)
            self.active_tool_window.show()
        except ImportError as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to load font tools: {e}")

    def open_cheats_manager(self):
        if not self.worker:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Emulation must be running to manage cheats.")
            return
        from cheats import CheatManagerWindow
        self.active_tool_window = CheatManagerWindow(self.worker.cheat_engine, self.config, self)
        self.active_tool_window.show()

    def open_controller_config(self):
        dlg = ControllerConfigDialog(self.config, self.controller_manager, self)
        if dlg.exec():
            self._build_keymap()

    def toggle_pixel_ruler(self):
        self.video_widget.ruler_enabled = self.ruler_action.isChecked()
        self.video_widget.show_osd("Pixel Ruler: " + ("ON" if self.ruler_action.isChecked() else "OFF"))
        self.video_widget.update()

    def _build_keymap(self):
        self.keymap = {}
        self.autofire_keymap = {}
        keyboard_cfg = self.config.get("input", "keyboard") or {}
        
        for gba_btn, user_key in keyboard_cfg.items():
            if gba_btn in LIBRETRO_BTNS:
                qt_key = QT_KEY_MAP.get(str(user_key).lower())
                if qt_key:
                    self.keymap[qt_key] = LIBRETRO_BTNS[gba_btn]

        for af_btn in ["autofire_a", "autofire_b", "autofire_l", "autofire_r"]:
            user_key = keyboard_cfg.get(af_btn)
            if user_key:
                qt_key = QT_KEY_MAP.get(str(user_key).lower())
                if qt_key:
                    self.autofire_keymap[qt_key] = af_btn.replace("autofire_", "")

    def poll_gamepad(self):
        if self.worker and hasattr(self, 'is_running') and self.is_running:
            self.controller_manager.poll_gamepad_events(self.worker)

    def load_rom(self):
        filters = "Supported ROMs (*.gba *.gb *.gbc *.sgb *.zip);;GBA ROMs (*.gba);;GB/GBC ROMs (*.gb *.gbc);;All Files (*.*)"
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Load Game", "", filters)
        if filepath:
            self.load_rom_file(filepath)

    def load_rom_file(self, filepath: str):
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "File Error", f"ROM not found:\n{filepath}")
            return
        self.current_rom = filepath
        self.config.add_recent_file(filepath)
        self.update_recent_menu()
        self.start_emulation()

    def start_emulation(self):
        if not os.path.exists(self.core_path):
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Core Error", f"Core not found:\n{self.core_path}")
            return

        if self.worker:
            self.worker.stop()
            self.worker.wait()

        self.worker = EmulatorWorker(self.core_path, self.current_rom, self.config)
        self.worker.frame_ready.connect(self.video_widget.set_frame)
        self.worker.error_occurred.connect(self.handle_error)
        self.worker.replay_finished.connect(self.on_replay_finished)
        
        # Connect Sensors, Vibration, and Identification Signals
        self.worker.rumble_triggered.connect(self.video_widget.on_rumble_triggered)
        self.worker.rumble_triggered.connect(self.controller_manager.trigger_rumble)
        self.worker.solar_changed.connect(self.on_solar_updated)
        self.worker.tilt_changed.connect(self.on_tilt_updated)
        self.worker.game_identified.connect(self.on_game_identified)
        
        self.worker.fast_forward = self.ff_action.isChecked()
        self.worker.is_paused = self.pause_action.isChecked()
        
        self.worker.start()
        self.is_running = True

    def reset_emulation(self):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.request_reset()
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Reset")

    def step_frame(self):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.step_one_frame()
            self.pause_action.setChecked(True)
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Stepped 1 Frame")

    def handle_error(self, err_msg):
        QMessageBox.warning(self, "Emulation Error", err_msg)

    def toggle_pause(self):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.is_paused = self.pause_action.isChecked()
            self.video_widget.show_osd("Paused" if self.worker.is_paused else "Resumed")

    def toggle_ff(self):
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.fast_forward = self.ff_action.isChecked()
            spd = float(self.config.get("emulation", "fast_forward_ratio") or 2.0)
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Fast Forward: {'ON (' + str(spd) + 'x)' if self.worker.fast_forward else 'OFF'}")

    def cycle_ff_speed(self, forward: bool = True):
        speeds = [1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 8.0, 10.0]
        cur = float(self.config.get("emulation", "fast_forward_ratio") or 2.0)
        closest_idx = min(range(len(speeds)), key=lambda i: abs(speeds[i] - cur))

        if forward:
            new_idx = min(len(speeds) - 1, closest_idx + 1)
        else:
            new_idx = max(0, closest_idx - 1)

        new_spd = speeds[new_idx]
        self.set_ff_speed(new_spd)
        if not self.ff_action.isChecked():
            self.ff_action.setChecked(True)
            self.toggle_ff()

    def toggle_skip_bios(self):
        enabled = self.skip_bios_action.isChecked()
        self.config.set("emulation", "skip_bios", enabled)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Skip BIOS: {'ON' if enabled else 'OFF'} (Applies on next load)")

    def set_ff_speed(self, ratio: float):
        self.config.set("emulation", "fast_forward_ratio", ratio)
        if ratio in self.ff_actions:
            self.ff_actions[ratio].setChecked(True)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Fast Forward Speed: {ratio}x")

    def toggle_interframe_blending(self):
        enabled = self.blend_action.isChecked()
        self.config.set("video", "interframe_blending", enabled)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Interframe Blending: {'ON' if enabled else 'OFF'}")

    def set_frameskip(self, count: int):
        self.config.set("video", "frameskip", count)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Frameskip: {count}")

    def set_active_slot(self, slot: int):
        self.current_slot = slot
        self.config.set("emulation", "state_slot", slot)
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Selected State Slot {slot}")

    def take_screenshot(self):
        if not self.worker or self.video_widget.current_frame_data is None:
            return
        out_dir = self.config.get("paths", "screenshots")
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
        filename = f"screenshot_{datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
        Image.fromarray(self.video_widget.current_frame_data, 'RGB').save(os.path.join(out_dir, filename))
        self.video_widget.show_osd("Screenshot Captured!")

    def get_state_path(self, slot: int):
        state_dir = self.config.get("paths", "states")
        os.makedirs(state_dir, exist_ok=True)
        base_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(self.current_rom))[0]
        return os.path.join(state_dir, f"{base_name}.ss{slot}")

    def quick_save_state(self, slot: int):
        if not self.worker:
            return
        self.worker.save_state(self.get_state_path(slot))
        self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Saved to Slot {slot}")

    def quick_load_state(self, slot: int):
        if not self.worker:
            return
        path = self.get_state_path(slot)
        if os.path.exists(path):
            self.worker.load_state(path)
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Loaded from Slot {slot}")
        else:
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Slot {slot} is empty!")

    def save_state_to_file(self):
        if not self.worker:
            return
        state_dir = self.config.get("paths", "states")
        os.makedirs(state_dir, exist_ok=True)
        base_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(self.current_rom))[0]
        default_path = os.path.join(state_dir, f"{base_name}.state")
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Save State to File", default_path, "Save States (*.state *.ss*)")
        if filepath:
            self.worker.save_state(filepath)
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Saved: {os.path.basename(filepath)}")

    def load_state_from_file(self):
        if not self.worker:
            return
        state_dir = self.config.get("paths", "states")
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Load State from File", state_dir, "Save States (*.state *.ss*)")
        if filepath:
            self.worker.load_state(filepath)
            self.video_widget.show_osd(f"Loaded: {os.path.basename(filepath)}")

    def keyPressEvent(self, event):
        if not self.worker:
            return

        # Fast-Forward Hotkeys: '=' (Toggle / Speed Up) and '-' (Toggle / Slow Down)
        if event.key() in [Qt.Key.Key_Equal, Qt.Key.Key_Plus] and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            if not self.ff_action.isChecked():
                self.ff_action.setChecked(True)
                self.toggle_ff()
            else:
                self.cycle_ff_speed(forward=True)
            return

        if event.key() in [Qt.Key.Key_Minus, Qt.Key.Key_Underscore] and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            if not self.ff_action.isChecked():
                self.ff_action.setChecked(True)
                self.toggle_ff()
            else:
                cur_ratio = float(self.config.get("emulation", "fast_forward_ratio") or 2.0)
                if abs(cur_ratio - 1.5) < 0.01:
                    self.ff_action.setChecked(False)
                    self.toggle_ff()
                else:
                    self.cycle_ff_speed(forward=False)
            return

        # Solar Sensor Hotkeys (Boktai)
        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_BracketRight:
            self.adjust_solar_sensor(1)
            return
        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_BracketLeft:
            self.adjust_solar_sensor(-1)
            return

        # Gyro / Tilt Simulation Hotkeys
        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Q:
            new_ang = (self.video_widget.current_gyro_angle - 15) % 360
            self.worker.set_gyro_angle(new_ang)
            return
        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_E:
            new_ang = (self.video_widget.current_gyro_angle + 15) % 360
            self.worker.set_gyro_angle(new_ang)
            return
        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Backspace:
            self.reset_sensors()
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_T:
            self.trigger_ai_translation()
            return

        if ((event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_D and 
             event.modifiers() == (Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier | Qt.KeyboardModifier.ShiftModifier)) 
            or event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_F11):
            self.trigger_instant_scene_dump()
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_G and event.modifiers() == Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier:
            self.open_graphic_editor()
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_H and event.modifiers() == Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier:
            self._open_dev_tool(MemoryViewerDialog)
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Tab and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            self.ff_action.setChecked(True)
            self.toggle_ff()
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_R and event.modifiers() == Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier:
            self.reset_emulation()
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_N and event.modifiers() == Qt.KeyboardModifier.ControlModifier:
            self.step_frame()
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_F10:
            if event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ShiftModifier:
                self.quick_save_state(self.current_slot)
            else:
                self.quick_load_state(self.current_slot)
            return

        if Qt.Key.Key_F1 <= event.key() <= Qt.Key.Key_F9:
            slot = event.key() - Qt.Key.Key_F1 + 1
            if event.modifiers() & Qt.KeyboardModifier.ShiftModifier:
                self.quick_save_state(slot)
            else:
                self.quick_load_state(slot)
            return

        af_btn = self.autofire_keymap.get(event.key())
        if af_btn and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            self.worker.set_autofire(af_btn, True)
            return

        btn_id = self.keymap.get(event.key())
        if btn_id is not None and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            self.worker.set_input(btn_id, True)

    def keyReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if not self.worker:
            return

        if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Tab and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            self.ff_action.setChecked(False)
            self.toggle_ff()
            return

        af_btn = self.autofire_keymap.get(event.key())
        if af_btn and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            self.worker.set_autofire(af_btn, False)
            return

        btn_id = self.keymap.get(event.key())
        if btn_id is not None and not event.isAutoRepeat():
            self.worker.set_input(btn_id, False)

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        self.is_running = False
        if hasattr(self, 'gamepad_timer'):
            self.gamepad_timer.stop()
        if self.worker:
            self.worker.stop()
            self.worker.wait()
        self.config.save()
        event.accept()
```

--- START OF FILE translation_tools.py ---

# translation_tools.py
import os
import struct
import csv
import re
import numpy as np
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, 
                             QLabel, QPushButton, QComboBox, QLineEdit, 
                             QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, 
                             QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QTabWidget, QGroupBox,
                             QTextEdit, QSpinBox, QCheckBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer

class EncodingManager:
    """Handles Shift-JIS, ASCII, and Custom Hex .tbl decoding and encoding."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.mode = "ASCII"
        self.custom_tbl = {}
        self.rev_tbl = {}
        self.rev_keys = []
        self.max_key_len = 1

    def load_tbl(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        self.custom_tbl.clear()
        self.rev_tbl.clear()
        self.rev_keys.clear()
        self.max_key_len = 1
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                for line in f:
                    line = line.strip()
                    if not line or '=' not in line: continue
                    hex_part, text_part = line.split('=', 1)
                    
                    try:
                        key_bytes = bytes.fromhex(hex_part)
                        self.custom_tbl[key_bytes] = text_part
                        if len(key_bytes) > self.max_key_len:
                            self.max_key_len = len(key_bytes)
                    except ValueError:
                        continue
            
            # Build reverse lookup table for encoding
            self.rev_tbl = {v: k for k, v in self.custom_tbl.items()}
            self.rev_keys = sorted(self.rev_tbl.keys(), key=len, reverse=True)
            self.mode = "Custom TBL"
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"TBL Load Error: {e}")
            return False

    def decode(self, data: bytes) -> str:
        if not data: return ""
        
        if self.mode == "Shift-JIS":
            return data.decode('shift_jis', errors='replace').replace('\n', ' ')
        elif self.mode == "ASCII":
            return "".join(chr(b) if 32 <= b <= 126 else "." for b in data)
        elif self.mode == "Custom TBL" and self.custom_tbl:
            decoded = ""
            i = 0
            while i < len(data):
                matched = False
                for length in range(self.max_key_len, 0, -1):
                    if i + length > len(data): continue
                    chunk = data[i:i+length]
                    if chunk in self.custom_tbl:
                        decoded += self.custom_tbl[chunk]
                        i += length
                        matched = True
                        break
                if not matched:
                    decoded += "."
                    i += 1
            return decoded
        return "".join(chr(b) if 32 <= b <= 126 else "." for b in data)

    def encode(self, text: str) -> bytes:
        if not text: return b""
        out = bytearray()
        
        parts = re.split(r'\[([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})\]', text)
        
        for i, part in enumerate(parts):
            if i % 2 == 1:
                out.append(int(part, 16))
            else:
                if not part: continue
                if self.mode == "Shift-JIS":
                    out.extend(part.encode('shift_jis', errors='ignore'))
                elif self.mode == "ASCII":
                    out.extend(part.encode('ascii', errors='ignore'))
                elif self.mode == "Custom TBL" and self.rev_tbl:
                    pi = 0
                    while pi < len(part):
                        matched = False
                        for k in self.rev_keys:
                            if part.startswith(k, pi):
                                out.extend(self.rev_tbl[k])
                                pi += len(k)
                                matched = True
                                break
                        if not matched:
                            out.extend(part[pi].encode('ascii', errors='ignore'))
                            pi += 1
        return bytes(out)


class TranslationToolsDialog(QDialog):
    """Integrated Translation Suite for ROM Hackers."""
    def __init__(self, worker, config, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.worker = worker
        self.config = config
        self.encoding = EncodingManager()
        
        self.setWindowTitle("Translation Tools - Text Inspector & Script Patcher")
        self.resize(1000, 700)
        
        self._init_ui()
        self.refresh_memory()

    def _init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        # --- Top Toolbar ---
        toolbar = QHBoxLayout()
        toolbar.addWidget(QLabel("<b>Global Encoding Mode:</b>"))
        
        self.enc_combo = QComboBox()
        self.enc_combo.addItems(["ASCII", "Shift-JIS", "Custom TBL"])
        self.enc_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_encoding_changed)
        toolbar.addWidget(self.enc_combo)
        
        self.load_tbl_btn = QPushButton("Load .tbl File...")
        self.load_tbl_btn.clicked.connect(self.on_load_tbl)
        toolbar.addWidget(self.load_tbl_btn)
        
        self.tbl_status = QLabel("No table loaded.")
        self.tbl_status.setStyleSheet("color: #888; font-style: italic;")
        toolbar.addWidget(self.tbl_status)
        toolbar.addStretch()
        main_layout.addLayout(toolbar)

        # --- Tabs ---
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        
        self.tab_viewer = QWidget()
        self._init_viewer_tab(self.tab_viewer)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_viewer, "Hex / Text Viewer")
        
        self.tab_script = QWidget()
        self._init_script_tab(self.tab_script)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_script, "Script Extractor & Auto-Repointer")

        self.tab_search = QWidget()
        self._init_search_tab(self.tab_search)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_search, "Relative Value Search")

        self.tab_injector = QWidget()
        self._init_injector_tab(self.tab_injector)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_injector, "WYSIWYG Live RAM Text Injector")
        
        main_layout.addWidget(self.tabs)

    # =========================================================================
    # TAB 1: HEX / TEXT VIEWER
    # =========================================================================
    def _init_viewer_tab(self, parent_widget):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(parent_widget)
        
        nav_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        nav_bar.addWidget(QLabel("Region:"))
        self.region_combo = QComboBox()
        self.region_combo.addItems(["ROM (Cartridge)", "EWRAM (256 KB)", "IWRAM (32 KB)"])
        self.region_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.refresh_memory)
        nav_bar.addWidget(self.region_combo)

        nav_bar.addWidget(QLabel("Offset (Hex):"))
        self.jump_edit = QLineEdit("0x00000000")
        self.jump_edit.setFixedWidth(100)
        nav_bar.addWidget(self.jump_edit)
        
        jump_btn = QPushButton("Jump")
        jump_btn.clicked.connect(self.on_jump)
        nav_bar.addWidget(jump_btn)
        nav_bar.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(nav_bar)

        self.hex_display = QLabel()
        self.hex_display.setStyleSheet("font-family: monospace; font-size: 13px; background: #111; color: #00FF66; padding: 10px;")
        self.hex_display.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)
        layout.addWidget(self.hex_display)

        btn_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        prev_btn = QPushButton("◀ Prev Page")
        prev_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self.navigate_page(-1))
        next_btn = QPushButton("Next Page ▶")
        next_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self.navigate_page(1))
        btn_bar.addWidget(prev_btn)
        btn_bar.addWidget(next_btn)
        layout.addLayout(btn_bar)
        
        self.current_offset = 0
        self.bytes_per_page = 16 * 23

    def get_region_data(self):
        sel = self.region_combo.currentText()
        if sel.startswith("ROM") and self.worker.rom_path:
            with open(self.worker.rom_path, 'rb') as f:
                return f.read(), 0x08000000
        elif sel.startswith("EWRAM"):
            return self.worker.get_memory_region("EWRAM"), 0x02000000
        elif sel.startswith("IWRAM"):
            mem = self.worker.get_memory_region("SYSTEM_RAM")
            if mem and len(mem) > 256 * 1024:
                return mem[256 * 1024:], 0x03000000
        return None, 0

    def on_jump(self):
        try:
            val = int(self.jump_edit.text(), 16)
            _, base_addr = self.get_region_data()
            if val >= base_addr: val -= base_addr
            self.current_offset = val
            self.refresh_memory()
        except ValueError: pass

    def navigate_page(self, direction):
        self.current_offset = max(0, self.current_offset + (direction * self.bytes_per_page))
        self.refresh_memory()

    def refresh_memory(self):
        raw_mem, base_addr = self.get_region_data()
        if not raw_mem:
            self.hex_display.setText("Region data not available.")
            return

        self.current_offset = min(max(0, self.current_offset), max(0, len(raw_mem) - self.bytes_per_page))
        chunk = raw_mem[self.current_offset:self.current_offset + self.bytes_per_page]

        lines = [f"Offset      00 01 02 03 04 05 06 07  08 09 0A 0B 0C 0D 0E 0F  [{self.encoding.mode}]"]
        lines.append("-" * 75)

        for i in range(0, len(chunk), 16):
            sub = chunk[i:i + 16]
            hex_part1 = " ".join(f"{b:02X}" for b in sub[:8])
            hex_part2 = " ".join(f"{b:02X}" for b in sub[8:])
            
            text_part = self.encoding.decode(bytes(sub))
            text_part = text_part.ljust(16)[:16] 
            
            line_addr = base_addr + self.current_offset + i
            lines.append(f"{line_addr:08X}    {hex_part1:<23}  {hex_part2:<23}  |{text_part}|")

        self.hex_display.setText("\n".join(lines))

    # =========================================================================
    # TAB 2: SCRIPT EXTRACTOR & AUTO-REPOINTER
    # =========================================================================
    def _init_script_tab(self, parent_widget):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(parent_widget)
        
        scan_grp = QGroupBox("1. Extract Scripts from Pointer Table")
        scan_layout = QHBoxLayout(scan_grp)
        
        scan_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Table Start:"))
        self.ptr_start_edit = QLineEdit("0x08000000")
        scan_layout.addWidget(self.ptr_start_edit)
        
        scan_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Table End:"))
        self.ptr_end_edit = QLineEdit("0x08000100")
        scan_layout.addWidget(self.ptr_end_edit)
        
        scan_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Terminator (Hex):"))
        self.ptr_term_edit = QLineEdit("00")
        self.ptr_term_edit.setFixedWidth(50)
        scan_layout.addWidget(self.ptr_term_edit)
        
        scan_btn = QPushButton("Scan & Dump Scripts")
        scan_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #2196F3; color: white; font-weight: bold;")
        scan_btn.clicked.connect(self.scan_pointers_for_scripts)
        scan_layout.addWidget(scan_btn)
        layout.addWidget(scan_grp)

        self.script_table = QTableWidget(0, 4)
        self.script_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Pointer Addr", "Target Addr", "Original Text", "Translated Text"])
        self.script_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.script_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        layout.addWidget(self.script_table)
        
        csv_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        export_csv_btn = QPushButton("⬇ Export to CSV")
        export_csv_btn.clicked.connect(self.export_scripts_csv)
        import_csv_btn = QPushButton("⬆ Import from CSV")
        import_csv_btn.clicked.connect(self.import_scripts_csv)
        csv_bar.addWidget(export_csv_btn)
        csv_bar.addWidget(import_csv_btn)
        csv_bar.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(csv_bar)

        patch_grp = QGroupBox("2. Repoint and Patch ROM")
        patch_layout = QHBoxLayout(patch_grp)
        
        patch_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Free Space Offset (Target for injected text):"))
        self.free_space_edit = QLineEdit("0x08800000")
        patch_layout.addWidget(self.free_space_edit)
        
        patch_btn = QPushButton("Translate & Generate Patched ROM...")
        patch_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4CAF50; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 5px;")
        patch_btn.clicked.connect(self.apply_translations_and_repoint)
        patch_layout.addWidget(patch_btn)
        layout.addWidget(patch_grp)

    def scan_pointers_for_scripts(self):
        if not self.worker.rom_path or not os.path.exists(self.worker.rom_path):
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "No ROM file available to scan.")
            return
            
        try:
            start_addr = int(self.ptr_start_edit.text(), 16)
            end_addr = int(self.ptr_end_edit.text(), 16)
            terminator = int(self.ptr_term_edit.text(), 16)
        except ValueError:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Input Error", "Please ensure addresses are valid Hex.")
            return
            
        with open(self.worker.rom_path, 'rb') as f:
            rom_data = f.read()
            
        rom_size = len(rom_data)
        if start_addr >= 0x08000000: start_addr -= 0x08000000
        if end_addr >= 0x08000000: end_addr -= 0x08000000
        
        self.script_table.setRowCount(0)
        
        row = 0
        for offset in range(start_addr, end_addr, 4):
            if offset + 4 > rom_size: break
            ptr_val = struct.unpack("<I", rom_data[offset:offset+4])[0]
            
            if 0x08000000 <= ptr_val < (0x08000000 + rom_size):
                str_offset = ptr_val - 0x08000000
                str_bytes = bytearray()
                curr = str_offset
                while curr < rom_size:
                    b = rom_data[curr]
                    if b == terminator: break
                    str_bytes.append(b)
                    curr += 1
                
                decoded_text = self.encoding.decode(bytes(str_bytes))
                
                self.script_table.insertRow(row)
                self.script_table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(f"0x{0x08000000 + offset:08X}"))
                self.script_table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(f"0x{ptr_val:08X}"))
                
                orig_item = QTableWidgetItem(decoded_text)
                orig_item.setFlags(orig_item.flags() & ~Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEditable)
                self.script_table.setItem(row, 2, orig_item)
                self.script_table.setItem(row, 3, QTableWidgetItem(""))
                row += 1
                
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Scan Complete", f"Successfully extracted {row} strings from pointers.")

    def export_scripts_csv(self):
        if self.script_table.rowCount() == 0: return
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export Scripts", "", "CSV Files (*.csv)")
        if filepath:
            with open(filepath, 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                writer = csv.writer(f)
                writer.writerow(["Pointer Addr", "Target Addr", "Original Text", "Translated Text"])
                for r in range(self.script_table.rowCount()):
                    writer.writerow([
                        self.script_table.item(r, 0).text(),
                        self.script_table.item(r, 1).text(),
                        self.script_table.item(r, 2).text(),
                        self.script_table.item(r, 3).text() if self.script_table.item(r, 3) else ""
                    ])
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Exported", f"Scripts saved to {os.path.basename(filepath)}")

    def import_scripts_csv(self):
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Import Scripts", "", "CSV Files (*.csv)")
        if filepath:
            self.script_table.setRowCount(0)
            with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                reader = csv.reader(f)
                next(reader, None)
                for row, data in enumerate(reader):
                    if len(data) < 4: continue
                    self.script_table.insertRow(row)
                    self.script_table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(data[0]))
                    self.script_table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(data[1]))
                    
                    orig_item = QTableWidgetItem(data[2])
                    orig_item.setFlags(orig_item.flags() & ~Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEditable)
                    self.script_table.setItem(row, 2, orig_item)
                    self.script_table.setItem(row, 3, QTableWidgetItem(data[3]))
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Imported", "Scripts loaded successfully.")

    def apply_translations_and_repoint(self):
        if self.script_table.rowCount() == 0: return
        
        try:
            free_space_addr = int(self.free_space_edit.text(), 16)
            terminator = int(self.ptr_term_edit.text(), 16)
        except ValueError:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Input Error", "Please ensure Free Space offset and Terminator are valid Hex.")
            return
            
        with open(self.worker.rom_path, 'rb') as f:
            rom_data = bytearray(f.read())
            
        if free_space_addr >= 0x08000000:
            free_space_offset = free_space_addr - 0x08000000
        else:
            free_space_offset = free_space_addr
            
        lines_patched = 0
        
        for r in range(self.script_table.rowCount()):
            trans_item = self.script_table.item(r, 3)
            if not trans_item or not trans_item.text().strip(): continue
                
            translated_text = trans_item.text()
            ptr_addr = int(self.script_table.item(r, 0).text(), 16) - 0x08000000
            
            encoded_bytes = self.encoding.encode(translated_text) + bytes([terminator])
            
            if free_space_offset + len(encoded_bytes) > len(rom_data):
                padding_needed = (free_space_offset + len(encoded_bytes)) - len(rom_data)
                rom_data.extend(b'\xFF' * padding_needed)
                
            rom_data[free_space_offset : free_space_offset + len(encoded_bytes)] = encoded_bytes
            new_ptr_val = free_space_offset + 0x08000000
            rom_data[ptr_addr : ptr_addr + 4] = struct.pack("<I", new_ptr_val)
            
            free_space_offset += len(encoded_bytes)
            lines_patched += 1

        default_name = f"translated_{os.path.basename(self.worker.rom_path)}"
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Save Patched ROM", default_name, "GBA ROMs (*.gba)")
        if save_path:
            with open(save_path, 'wb') as f: f.write(rom_data)
            self.free_space_edit.setText(f"0x{free_space_offset + 0x08000000:08X}")
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"Repointed and injected {lines_patched} translated lines into new ROM!")

    # =========================================================================
    # TAB 3: RELATIVE VALUE SEARCH
    # =========================================================================
    def _init_search_tab(self, parent_widget):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(parent_widget)
        
        desc = QLabel("<b>Relative Search:</b> Find text with unknown custom encodings by searching for mathematical character distances.")
        layout.addWidget(desc)
        
        input_bar = QHBoxLayout()
        input_bar.addWidget(QLabel("Search String:"))
        self.search_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.search_edit.setPlaceholderText("e.g. SWORD")
        input_bar.addWidget(self.search_edit)
        
        search_btn = QPushButton("Search ROM")
        search_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #2196F3; color: white; font-weight: bold;")
        search_btn.clicked.connect(self.run_relative_search)
        input_bar.addWidget(search_btn)
        layout.addLayout(input_bar)

        self.search_table = QTableWidget(0, 3)
        self.search_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["ROM Address", "Guessed Mapping Offset", "Generate Custom .tbl"])
        self.search_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        layout.addWidget(self.search_table)

    def on_encoding_changed(self):
        self.encoding.mode = self.enc_combo.currentText()
        self.refresh_memory()

    def on_load_tbl(self):
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Load Table File", "", "Table Files (*.tbl);;All Files (*.*)")
        if filepath and self.encoding.load_tbl(filepath):
            self.tbl_status.setText(f"Loaded: {os.path.basename(filepath)}")
            idx = self.enc_combo.findText("Custom TBL")
            if idx >= 0: self.enc_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
            self.refresh_memory()

    def run_relative_search(self):
        query = self.search_edit.text().strip()
        if len(query) < 3:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Notice", "Please enter at least 3 characters for relative search.")
            return
            
        rom_data, base_addr = self.get_region_data()
        if not rom_data: return

        target_diffs = [(ord(query[i+1]) - ord(query[i])) % 256 for i in range(len(query)-1)]
        
        data_np = np.frombuffer(rom_data, dtype=np.uint8).astype(np.int16)
        diffs_np = (data_np[1:] - data_np[:-1]) % 256
        
        L = len(diffs_np) - len(target_diffs) + 1
        mask = (diffs_np[:L] == target_diffs[0])
        for j in range(1, len(target_diffs)):
            mask &= (diffs_np[j : L+j] == target_diffs[j])
            
        match_indices = np.where(mask)[0]
        
        self.search_table.setRowCount(len(match_indices))
        for row, idx in enumerate(match_indices):
            addr = base_addr + idx
            
            base_char_val = ord(query[0])
            matched_byte_val = rom_data[idx]
            mapping_offset = (matched_byte_val - base_char_val)
            
            self.search_table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(f"0x{addr:08X}"))
            self.search_table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(f"Offset: {mapping_offset:+}"))
            
            gen_btn = QPushButton("Generate .tbl & View")
            gen_btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, a=addr, off=mapping_offset: self.generate_tbl_and_jump(a, off))
            self.search_table.setCellWidget(row, 2, gen_btn)
            
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Search Complete", f"Found {len(match_indices)} potential matches in ROM.")

    def generate_tbl_and_jump(self, address, offset):
        tbl_content = ""
        chars_to_map = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789!?.,'- "
        for char in chars_to_map:
            hex_val = (ord(char) + offset) % 256
            tbl_content += f"{hex_val:02X}={char}\n"
            
        export_dir = self.config.get("paths", "exports") or "exports"
        tbl_path = os.path.join(export_dir, "auto_generated.tbl")
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(tbl_path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(tbl_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(tbl_content)
            
        self.encoding.load_tbl(tbl_path)
        self.tbl_status.setText("Loaded: auto_generated.tbl")
        
        idx = self.enc_combo.findText("Custom TBL")
        if idx >= 0: self.enc_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        
        self.region_combo.setCurrentIndex(0) 
        self.current_offset = (address - 0x08000000) & ~0xF
        self.refresh_memory()
        
        self.tabs.setCurrentIndex(0)

    # =========================================================================
    # TAB 4: WYSIWYG LIVE RAM TEXT INJECTOR (WITH AUTO-DETECT)
    # =========================================================================
    def _init_injector_tab(self, parent_widget):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(parent_widget)
        
        desc = QLabel(
            "<b>WYSIWYG Live Text Injector</b><br>"
            "Type translated text to inject directly into the game's live RAM (EWRAM/IWRAM) buffer.<br>"
            "<i>Note: Use [XX] for raw hex control codes (e.g., [FE] for Newline, [FF] for String Terminator).</i><br>"
            "Draw a box around the on-screen dialogue using the <b>Screen Pixel Ruler (Translation Menu)</b>, then click Auto-Detect!"
        )
        desc.setStyleSheet("background-color: #1a1a1a; padding: 10px; border-radius: 5px;")
        layout.addWidget(desc)

        cfg_row = QHBoxLayout()
        cfg_row.addWidget(QLabel("Target RAM Address:"))
        self.inj_addr_edit = QLineEdit("0x02000000")
        self.inj_addr_edit.setFixedWidth(120)
        cfg_row.addWidget(self.inj_addr_edit)

        # 🎯 The Auto-Detect Button
        btn_detect = QPushButton("🎯 Auto-Detect from Ruler Box")
        btn_detect.setStyleSheet("background-color: #FF9800; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 5px;")
        btn_detect.clicked.connect(self.auto_detect_from_ruler)
        cfg_row.addWidget(btn_detect)

        cfg_row.addWidget(QLabel("Max Bytes:"))
        self.inj_max_bytes = QSpinBox()
        self.inj_max_bytes.setRange(1, 1024)
        self.inj_max_bytes.setValue(256)
        cfg_row.addWidget(self.inj_max_bytes)
        
        self.inj_auto_freeze = QCheckBox("Lock in RAM (15Hz)")
        self.inj_auto_freeze.toggled.connect(self.toggle_auto_inject)
        cfg_row.addWidget(self.inj_auto_freeze)
        cfg_row.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(cfg_row)

        self.inj_text_edit = QTextEdit()
        self.inj_text_edit.setPlaceholderText("Enter translated dialogue here...\nExample: Hello World![FE]This is a test.[FF]")
        layout.addWidget(self.inj_text_edit)

        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        inj_btn = QPushButton("⚡ Inject Text to RAM")
        inj_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #9C27B0; color: white; font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px; padding: 10px;")
        inj_btn.clicked.connect(self.inject_text_to_ram)
        btn_row.addWidget(inj_btn)
        
        self.inj_status = QLabel("Ready.")
        self.inj_status.setStyleSheet("color: #00FF66; font-weight: bold;")
        btn_row.addWidget(self.inj_status)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

        self.auto_inject_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.auto_inject_timer.setInterval(66)
        self.auto_inject_timer.timeout.connect(self.inject_text_to_ram)

    def auto_detect_from_ruler(self):
        """Uses the drawn Screen Ruler box to auto-detect the RAM address of the text inside."""
        if not self.parent() or not hasattr(self.parent(), 'video_widget'):
            return

        video_widget = self.parent().video_widget
        if not video_widget.ruler_start or not video_widget.ruler_end:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Please draw a box over the text on the screen using the Pixel Ruler (Translation Menu) first.")
            return

        x1, y1 = video_widget.ruler_start
        x2, y2 = video_widget.ruler_end
        rx, ry = min(x1, x2), min(y1, y2)
        rw, rh = abs(x2 - x1), abs(y2 - y1)

        # Convert screen pixels to tilemap coordinates (8x8)
        tx1, ty1 = rx // 8, ry // 8
        tx2, ty2 = (rx + rw) // 8, (ry + rh) // 8

        vram = self.worker.get_memory_region("VRAM")
        if not vram: return

        # Find active screen base blocks
        active_sbbs = []
        for sbb in range(32):
            if any(vram[sbb*0x800 : (sbb+1)*0x800]):
                active_sbbs.append(sbb)

        best_match = None
        for sbb in active_sbbs:
            sbb_offset = sbb * 0x800
            map_entries = struct.unpack("<1024H", vram[sbb_offset:sbb_offset+0x800])
            
            # Extract longest contiguous horizontal sequence of non-zero tiles
            longest_seq = []
            for ty in range(ty1, ty2 + 1):
                current_seq = []
                for tx in range(tx1, tx2 + 1):
                    if ty < 32 and tx < 32:
                        t_idx = map_entries[ty * 32 + tx] & 0x3FF
                        if t_idx != 0:
                            current_seq.append(t_idx)
                        else:
                            if len(current_seq) > len(longest_seq):
                                longest_seq = current_seq
                            current_seq = []
                if len(current_seq) > len(longest_seq):
                    longest_seq = current_seq

            if len(longest_seq) >= 3:
                # Calculate relative distances of the tiles
                target_diffs = [(longest_seq[i+1] - longest_seq[i]) % 256 for i in range(len(longest_seq)-1)]
                
                # Fast relative search through EWRAM
                ewram = self.worker.get_memory_region("EWRAM")
                match = self._numpy_relative_search(ewram, target_diffs)
                if match is not None:
                    best_match = 0x02000000 + match
                    break
                
                # Fallback to IWRAM
                iwram = self.worker.get_memory_region("SYSTEM_RAM")
                if iwram and len(iwram) > 256 * 1024:
                    match = self._numpy_relative_search(iwram[256 * 1024:], target_diffs)
                    if match is not None:
                        best_match = 0x03000000 + match
                        break

        if best_match:
            self.inj_addr_edit.setText(f"0x{best_match:08X}")
            self.inj_status.setText(f"Auto-Detected target at 0x{best_match:08X}!")
        else:
            self.inj_status.setText("Could not find matching text sequence in RAM.")
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Not Found", "Could not correlate the screen tiles to an active string in RAM.\nThe game may be using VWF (Variable Width Fonts) or a 2-byte encoding.")

    def _numpy_relative_search(self, mem, target_diffs):
        if not mem or len(mem) < len(target_diffs): return None
        data_np = np.frombuffer(mem, dtype=np.uint8).astype(np.int16)
        diffs_np = (data_np[1:] - data_np[:-1]) % 256
        L = len(diffs_np) - len(target_diffs) + 1
        if L <= 0: return None
        
        mask = (diffs_np[:L] == target_diffs[0])
        for j in range(1, len(target_diffs)):
            mask &= (diffs_np[j : L+j] == target_diffs[j])
            
        indices = np.where(mask)[0]
        if len(indices) > 0:
            return indices[0]
        return None

    def toggle_auto_inject(self, checked):
        if checked:
            self.auto_inject_timer.start()
            self.inj_status.setText("Status: AUTO-INJECTING (Locked in RAM)")
        else:
            self.auto_inject_timer.stop()
            self.inj_status.setText("Status: Auto-Inject Stopped.")

    def inject_text_to_ram(self):
        if not self.worker or not self.worker.emu:
            self.inj_status.setText("Error: Emulator not running.")
            return

        try:
            target_addr = int(self.inj_addr_edit.text(), 16)
        except ValueError:
            self.inj_status.setText("Error: Invalid Target Address.")
            return

        text = self.inj_text_edit.toPlainText()
        if not text:
            return

        payload = self.encoding.encode(text)
        max_bytes = self.inj_max_bytes.value()

        if len(payload) > max_bytes:
            payload = payload[:max_bytes]

        if 0x02000000 <= target_addr <= 0x0203FFFF:
            offset = target_addr - 0x02000000
            self.worker.write_memory_region("EWRAM", offset, payload)
            if not self.inj_auto_freeze.isChecked():
                self.inj_status.setText(f"Success: Injected {len(payload)} bytes to EWRAM.")
        elif 0x03000000 <= target_addr <= 0x03007FFF:
            offset = target_addr - 0x03000000
            mem = self.worker.get_memory_region("SYSTEM_RAM")
            if mem and len(mem) > 256 * 1024:
                # Assuming IWRAM comes after EWRAM in the SYSTEM_RAM block
                self.worker.write_memory_region("SYSTEM_RAM", (256 * 1024) + offset, payload)
                if not self.inj_auto_freeze.isChecked():
                    self.inj_status.setText(f"Success: Injected {len(payload)} bytes to IWRAM.")
            else:
                self.inj_status.setText("Error: IWRAM region not accessible on this core.")
        else:
            self.inj_status.setText("Error: Address must be in EWRAM (0x02..) or IWRAM (0x03..).")

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if hasattr(self, 'auto_inject_timer'):
            self.auto_inject_timer.stop()
        event.accept()

--- END OF FILE translation_tools.py ---
